# Rashi on Ecclesiastes

רש"י על קהלת

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes)

הפירוש הנפוץ והמוכר ביותר לתורה, המבאר את פשטי המקראות בתוספת הרחבות פרשניות; חובר במאה ה-11.

Most widely-read biblical commentary, compiled in the 11th-century, explaining the simple meaning of the text with interpretive elaborations.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Rishonim on Tanakh/Rashi/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): The Metsudah Five Megillot, Lakewood, N.J., 2001 (Segments: 598, License: CC-BY)

Version (En): The Metsudah Five Megillot, Lakewood, N.J., 2001 (Segments: 598)


## Chapter 1


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:1:1)


**דִּבְרֵי קֹהֶלֶת.** כָּל־מָקוֹם שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר "דִּבְרֵי" אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא דִבְרֵי תוֹכֵחוֹת. "אֵלֶּה הַדְּבָרִים אֲשֶׁר דִּבֶּר משֶׁה", "וַיִּשְׁמַן יְשֻׁרוּן". "דִּבְרֵי עָמוֹס", "שִׁמְעוּ הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה פָּרוֹת הַבָּשָׁן". "דִּבְרֵי יִרְמְיָהוּ", "שַׁאֲלוּ־נָא וּרְאוּ אִם־יֹלֵד זָכָר וְגוֹ'". "אֵלֶּה דִּבְרֵי דָוִד", "וּבְלִיַּעַל כְּקוֹץ מֻנָד כֻּלָּהַם". "דִּבְרֵי קֹהֶלֶת", "וְזָרַח הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ . . . כָּל־הַנְּחָלִים הוֹלְכִים אֶל־הַיָּם". כִּנָּה אֶת־הָרְשָׁעִים בְּחַמָּה וּלְבָנָה וְיָם, שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם מַתְּנַת שָׂכָר. כַּךְ שְׁנוּיָה בְסִפְרֵי. וְלָמַדְתִּי מִשָּׁם שֶׁהָעִנְיָן מְדַבֵּר בָּרְשָׁעִים, וְהִמְשִׁילָם לְתִגְבֹּרֶת הַחַמָּה, שֶׁסּוֹפָהּ שׁוֹקַעַת. תּוֹסֶפֶת: דָּבָר אַחֵר: "כָּל־הַנְּחָלִים הוֹלְכִים אֶל־הַיָּם". מַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר? בְּעוֹבְדֵי עֲבוֹדַת אֱלִילִים נֶאֱמַר: שׁוֹטִים הַמִּשְׁתַּחֲוִים לַמַּיִם, סְבוּרִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶם מַמָּשׁ לְפִי שֶׁרוֹאִין אֶת־הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל, שֶׁכָּל־הַנְּחָלִים הוֹלְכִים בּוֹ, וְהוּא אֵינוֹ מָלֵא, וְאֵינָן יוֹדְעִין לְהָבִין כִּי אֶל הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁהַנְּחָלִים הוֹלְכִים, שָׁם הֵם שָׁבִים לָלֶכֶת, שֶׁמֵּימֵי הַנְּהָרוֹת הַהוֹלְכִים לְתוֹךְ הַיָּם, הֵם הַמַּיִם עַצְמָם שֶׁהָלְכוּ כְבָר. הֵם נוֹבְעוֹת מִתַּחַת הַתְּהוֹם וְהוֹלְכִים לְמַעְלָה מִן הַקַּרְקַע עַד הַיָּם וְחוֹזְרִים וְנוֹבְעִים; לְפִיכָךְ אֵין הַנְּהָרוֹת פּוֹסְקִים וְאֵין הַיָּם מָלֵא, וְלֹא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶן מַמָּשׁ. עַד כַּאן:


**The words of Koheles.** Wherever it is stated, “the words of דִּבְרֵי,” it introduces words or reproof. [For example,] “These are the words הַדְּבָרִים that Moshe spoke,”¹ [followed by,] “And Yeshurun became fat [and kicked].”² [Also,] “The words דִּבְרֵי of Amos,”³ [followed by,] “Hear this word, you cows of Bashan.”⁴ [Also,] “The words דִּבְרֵי of Yirmiyahu,”⁵ [followed by,] “Ask now and see whether a male gives birth, etc.”⁶ [And finally,] “And these are the words דִּבְרֵי of Dovid,”⁷ [followed by,] “But the wicked are all like thorns thrust away.”⁸ “The words דִּבְרֵי of Koheles” [is followed by,] “The sun rises ... All the rivers flow into the sea.”⁹ He refers to the wicked as the “sun,” the “moon,”¹⁰ and the “sea,” which earn no reward. So it was taught in Sifre. I learned from there that the section deals with the wicked and he compares them to the rising sun, which ultimately sets. Addendum: Another explanation, “All the rivers flow into the sea.” What is the meaning of this? This is stated concerning idolaters, the fools who prostrate themselves to the water thinking that it has substance because they see the Great Sea, into which all the rivers flow and it is not full, but they have no understanding that to the place where the rivers flow, they repeatedly go, for the water of the rivers that flow into the sea are the very same waters that already flowed; they flow from under the deep and flow above the surface until the sea and then flow again. Therefore, the rivers do not stop, and the sea is not full, and not because they have substance. End of addendum.

footnotes:
¹ Devarim 1:1.
² Ibid. 32:15.
³ Amos 1:1.
⁴ Ibid. 4:1.
⁵ Yirmiyahu 1:1.
⁶ Ibid. 30:6.
⁷ II Shmuel 23:1.
⁸ Ibid. 23:6.
⁹ Below, verse 5.
¹⁰ Below, verse 7.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:1:2)


**קֹהֶלֶת.** עַל־שֵׁם שֶׁקִּהֵל חָכְמוֹת הַרְבֵּה, וְכֵן בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר קוֹרֵהוּ אָגוּר בִּן־יָקֵה, שֶׁאָגַר כָּל־הַחָכְמָה וַהֲקִיאָהּ וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים שֶׁהָיָה אוֹמֵר כָּל־דְבָרָיו בְהַקְהֵל:


**Koheles.** [He was called Koheles] because he gathered [=קִהֵל much wisdom,¹¹ and similarly, elsewhere [Scripture] calls him, “Agur Bin Yokeh,”¹² because he gathered אָגַר all the wisdom and spewed וַהֲקִיאָהּ it out, and some say that he would say all his words in public assembly [=בְּהַקְהֵל.¹³

footnotes:
¹¹ The term קהלת meaning gathering is in Devarim 33:4 where it states, “the gathering [קהלת] of Yaakov.” (Metsudas Tzion)
¹² Mishlei 30:1. This is a reference to Shlomo, meaning the gatherer [אגור] of wisdom [בן] spewed out [יקה] the prophecy [המשא].
¹³ Alternatively, he gathered together and clarified many conflicting views. (Metsudas Tzion)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:1:3)


**מֶלֶךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָםִ.** עִיר הַחָכְמָה:


**King in Yerusholayim.** The city of wisdom.¹⁴ Koheles is replete with wisdom, therefore it states that its author reigned in “the city of wisdom,” thus he was exposed to much wisdom and his conclusions are worthy of great regard.

footnotes:
¹⁴ Shlomo is known by three titles. In Shir Hashirim 3:11 he is called King Shlomo, in Mishlei 1:1 he is called “King of Yisroel,” and here he is called “King in Yerusholayim.” The Gemara in Maseches Sanhedrin 20b, comments that his kingdom was diminished by the virtue of sin. He was first recognized universally as King Shlomo, after his sin he was King of Yisroel only, and then King of Yerusholayim alone. Alternatively, Sefer



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:2:1)


**הֲבֵל הֲבָלִים אָמַר קֹהֶלֶת.** קֹהֶלֶת קוֹרֵא תַגַּר וְאוֹמֵר עַל־כָּל־יְצִירַת שִׁבְעַת יְמֵי בְרֵאשִׁית שֶׁהַכֹּל הֶבֶל שֶׁל הֲבָלִים הוּא:


**Vanity of vanities, said Koheles.** Koheles complains about the entire seven days of creation, that it is all a vanity¹⁵ of vanities.

footnotes:
¹⁵ Vanity is something empty of substance, it is zero. However, when combined with something of substance, such as Torah study, it adds value to it, in the same manner that a zero that is placed next to a number adds value to it. (Mashal Umlitzah)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:2:2)


**הֲבֵל.** "הֲבֵל" נָקוּד חֲטָף פַּתָּח לְפִי שֶׁהוּא דָּבוּק, כְּלוֹמַר, הֶבֶל שֶׁבַּהֲבָלִים. שִׁבְעַת הֲבָלִים כְּנֶגֶד מַעֲשֵׂה שִׁבְעַת יְמֵי בְרֵאשִׁית:


**Vanity of.** הֲבֵל¹⁶] of ‘הבל’ is 37, corresponding to the 37 years of Shlomo’s reign, considering his exile of three years. (Gra) is punctuated with a *chataph patach*, because it is in the construct state; i.e., the vanity of the vanities. Seven vanities [are mentioned],¹⁷ corresponding to the seven days of creation.

footnotes:
¹⁶ The numerical value [gematria
¹⁷ הבל is one, הבלים [plural] are two,  הבל is one, הבלים are two, and הבל is one, making a total of seven.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:3:1)


**מַה־יִתְרוֹן.** שָׂכָר וּמוֹתָר:


**What profit.** [What] reward or gain.¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁸ Ordinarily, יתרון connotes “greater,” as when comparing two parts or objects, one to another. (Sifsei Chachomim)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:3:2)


**תַּחַת הַשָּׁמֶשׁ.** תְּמוּרַת הַתּוֹרָה שֶׁהִיא קְרוּיָה אוֹר, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר "וְתוֹרָה אוֹר", כָּל־עָמָל שֶׁהוּא מַחֲלִיף בּוֹ אֶת־עֵסֶק הַתּוֹרָה, מַה־שָׂכָר בּוֹ?:


**Beneath the sun.** Instead [=תַּחַת¹⁹.” The Gemara in Maseches Shabbos 30b renders תחת as “after,” and interprets the verse, that for his labor “after the sun” he realizes no profit, but for his labor “before the sun,” i.e., in Torah, which existed before God created the world, there is profit. (Sifsei Chachomim) of the Torah, which is called light,²⁰ as it is stated, “and the Torah is light.”²¹ All the labor which he does instead of engaging in Torah study, what reward does it yield?

footnotes:
¹⁹ Ordinarily, תחת means “under,” but in this verse that definition would be difficult. Therefore, Rashi interprets תחת as “instead,” as we find in Bereishis 30:15, “in exchange [תחת] for your son’s duda’im
²⁰ Mishlei 6:23.
²¹ “For all his labor” should not be misconstrued to include even engaging in Torah study, because otherwise Scripture should have stated בכל עמל [=all labor], however it states בכל עמלו [=his labor], referring to his own personal physical labor. Thus, one should labor in the study of Torah.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:4:1)


**דּוֹר הֹלֵךְ וְדוֹר בָּא.** כָּל־מַה שֶׁהָרָשָׁע יָגֵעַ וְעָמֵל לַעֲשׁק וְלִגְזֹל אֵינוֹ מְבַלֶּה אֶת־מַעֲשָׂיו, כִּי הַדּוֹר הוֹלֵךְ וְדוֹר אַחֵר בָּא וְנוֹטֵל הַכֹּל מִיַּד בָּנָיו, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "בָּנָיו יְרַצּוּ דַלִּים":


**A generation passes away and [another] generation comes.** As much as the wicked man toils and labors to oppress and to rob, he does not outlive his activities, for the generation passes on and another generation comes and takes away everything from his sons, as the matter is stated, “His children must appease the poor.”²²

footnotes:
²² Iyov 20:10.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:4:2)


**וְהָאָרֶץ לְעוֹלָם עֹמָדֶת.** וּמִי הֵם הַמִּתְקַיְּמִים? הָעֲנָוִים הַנְּמוּכִים, הַמַּגִּיעִין עַצְמָם עַד לָאָרֶץ, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר "וַעֲנָוִים יִרְשׁוּ אָרֶץ". וּמִדְרַשׁ תַּנְחוּמָא אוֹמֵר: כָּל־צַדִּיקֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל נִקְרְאוּ אֶרֶץ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "כִּי תִהְיוּ אַתֶּם אֶרֶץ חֵפֶץ":


**And the earth abides forever.** But who are the ones who [cause the earth to] endure? The humble and low, who bring themselves down to the ground, as the matter is stated, “But the humble shall inherit the earth.”²³ ²⁴ But Midrash Tanchuma states, that all the righteous of Yisroel are called earth [=אֶרֶץ, as it is stated, “for you will be a desirable land.”²⁵

footnotes:
²³ Tehillim 37:11.
²⁴ Alternatively, unlike generations, that when one passes by, another one comes, the earth does not change.
²⁵ Malachi 3:12.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:5:1)


**וְזָרַח הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ וְגוֹ'.** דּוֹר הֹלֵךְ וְדוֹר בָּא, כַּאֲשֶׁר הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ תִּזְרַח שַׁחֲרִית וְתִשְׁקַע עַרְבִית וְתֵלֵךְ כָּל־הַלַּיְלָה, שׁוֹאֶפֶת לָשׁוּב אֶל־מְקוֹם אֲשֶׁר זָרְחָה אֶתְמוֹל מִשָּׁם, שֶׁתִּזְרַח שָׁם גַּם הַיּוֹם:


**The sun rises, etc.** A generation passes on and a generation comes, as the sun rises in the morning and sets in the evening,²⁶ in ‘וזרח’ demonstrates the connection with the previous verse of generations passing by. and it goes throughout the night, yearning to return to the place from where it rose yesterday, that it will rise from there also today.

footnotes:
²⁶ The connective vav



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:6:1)


**הָרוּחַ.** רוּחוֹ שֶׁל שֶׁמֶשׁ, טלנ"ט בְּלַעַ"ז, כְּמוֹ "עַל אֲשֶׁר יִהְיֶה־שָּׁמָּה הָרוּחַ לָלֶכֶת":


**The wind.** The sun’s desire,²⁷ *talent* in O.F., as in, “toward wherever there was the will הָרוּחַ to go.”²⁸

footnotes:
²⁷ Ordinarily רוח means wind, but because the “sun” is the subject of this verse, Rashi defines רוח as “desire” or “will,” i.e., the sun’s desire. (Sifsei Chachomim)
²⁸ Yechezkeil 1:12.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:6:2)


**וְעַל־סְבִיבֹתָיו שָׁב.** גַּם מָחָר. כָּל־הֶקֶּף וְסִבּוּב שֶׁסָבַב אֶתְמוֹל, הוּא מַקִּיף וְסוֹבֵב הַיּוֹם:


**And upon its circuits [the wind] returns.** Also tomorrow, the entire circuit and encircling that it went around yesterday, it encircles and goes around today.²⁹

footnotes:
²⁹ The expression הולך [goes] is used for moving toward the south [symbolic of wisdom] and סובב [goes round, circles] is used for moving toward the north [symbolic of wealth]. הולך denotes going without purpose whereas סובב implies a determined action. Koheles hints that man pursues wisdom with indifference, whereas his pursuit of wealth he does with great effort and determination. (Toldos Aharon)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:6:3)


**הוֹלֵךְ אֶל־דָּרוֹם.** לְעוֹלָם בַּיּוֹם:


**It [the wind] goes toward the south.** Always by day.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:6:4)


**וְסוֹבֵב אֶל־צָפוֹן.** לְעוֹלָם בַּלַּיְלָה:


**And it turns about toward the north.** Always by night.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:6:5)


**סוֹבֵב סֹבֵב הוֹלֵךְ.** אֶל־פְּנֵי מִזְרָח וּמַעֲרָב, שֶׁפְּעָמִים מְהַלַּכְתָּן בַּיּוֹם, וּפְעָמִים מְסַבַּבְתָּן בַּלָּיְלָה. בְּתַמּוּז מְהַלַּכְתָּן, וּבְטֵבֵת סוֹבַבְתָּן. אַף הָרְשָׁעִים, כָּל־מַה שֶּׁשִּׁמְשָׁן זוֹרַחַת סוֹפָן לִשְׁקֹעַ. כָּל־מַה שֶּׁהֵם הוֹלְכִים וּמִתְגַּבְּרִים, סוֹפָם לָשׁוּב אֶל מְקוֹם צַחֲנָתָם. מִמְּקוֹם הַטִּנֹּפֶת בָּאוּ, וְלִמְקוֹם הַטִּנֹּפֶת יֵלֵכוּ. וְכֵן:


**Round and round goes [the wind].** To the eastern and western sides, which it sometimes goes through by day, and sometimes circles at night. In Tammuz it goes through them, and in Teves it circles around them. Also the wicked, no matter how much their sun rises, they will ultimately set. No matter how much they gain power, they will ultimately return to their stench. From filth they came, and to filth they will return. And so —



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:7:1)


**כָּל־הַנְּחָלִים הֹלְכִים אֶל־הַיָּם וְהַיָּם אֵינֶנוּ מָלֵא.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָן נִשְׁאָרִים בְּתוֹכוֹ, כִּי יַם אוֹקְיָנוֹס הוּא גָבוֹהַּ מִכָּל־הָעוֹלָם כֻּלּוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "הַקּוֹרֵא לְמֵי־הַיָּם וַיִּשְׁפְּכֵם וְגוֹ'". וּמֵהֵיכָן אָדָם שׁוֹפֵךְ? מִלְּמַעְלָה לְמַטָּה. וְהַנְּחָלִים הוֹלְכִים בִּמְחִלּוֹת תַּחַת הֶהָרִים מֵאוֹקְיָנוֹס, וְחוֹזְרִים וְנוֹבְעִים. וְזֶהוּ: "אֶל־מְקוֹם שֶׁהַנְּחָלִים הֹלְכִים שָׁם הֵם שָׁבִים". אַף הָרָשָׁע, כָּל־עֻמַּת שֶׁבָּא כֵּן יֵלֵךְ:


**All the rivers flow into the sea, yet the sea is not full.** Because they do not remain therein, for the ocean is higher than the entire world, as it is stated, “He summons the waters of the sea and pours them, etc.”³⁰ Now from where does a person pour? From above downward, and the rivers flow in the tunnels under the mountains from the ocean and flow repeatedly,³¹ and this is [the meaning of], “to the place where the rivers flow, there they continue [to flow].” Also, the wicked man, wherever he came from, so he will go.

footnotes:
³⁰ Amos 5:8.
³¹ They continue this cycle perpetually.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:8:1)


**כָּל־הַדְּבָרִים יְגֵעִים וְגוֹ' לֹא תִשְׂבַּע עַיִן וְלֹא תִמָּלֵא אֹזֶן.** מוּסָב עַל "מַה־יִתְרוֹן". אִם מַחֲלִיף הוּא עֵסֶק הַתּוֹרָה לְדַבֵּר בִּדְבָרִים בְּטֵלִים, הֲרֵי הֵם יְגֵעִים, וְלֹא יוּכַל לְהַשִּׂיג אֶת־כֻּלָּם, וְאִם בְּמַרְאִית הָעַיִן בָּא לַעֲסֹק, עֵינוֹ לֹא תִשְׂבַּע, וְאִם בִּשְׁמִיעַת הָאֹזֶן, אָזְנוֹ לֹא תִמָּלֵא:


**All things lead to weariness, etc., the eye is not satisfied [with seeing], nor the ear filled [with hearing].** This refers back to, “What profit [does man have],”³² if he exchanges the study of Torah to engage in idle talk? They are wearying, and he will not be able to acquire them all, and if he comes to indulge himself visually, his eyes will not be satisfied, and if he seeks aural satisfaction, his ears will not be gratified.³³

footnotes:
³² Above, verse 3.
³³ Rashi translates דברים as “words.” Alternatively,  דברים means “things,” i.e., “all things lead to weariness.” (Metsudas Dovid)



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:9:1)


**מַה שֶּׁהָיָה הוּא שֶׁיִּהְיֶה וְגוֹ'.** בְּכָל־מַה שֶּׁהוּא לָמֵד בְּדָבָר שֶׁהוּא חֲלִיפֵי הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ, אֵין בּוֹ חִדּוּשׁ; לֹא יִרְאֶה אֶלָּא מָה שֶּׁהָיָה כְבָר, שֶׁנִּבְרָא בְשֵׁשֶׁת יְמֵי בְרֵאשִׁית. אֲבָל הַהוֹגֶה בַתּוֹרָה, מוֹצֵא בָהּ תָּמִיד חִדּוּשֵׁי טְעָמִים, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "דַּדֶּיהָ יְרַוּוּךָ בְכָל־עֵת". מָה הַדַּד הַזֶּה, כָּל־זְמַן שֶׁהַתִּינוֹק מְמַשְׁמֵשׁ בּוֹ מוֹצֵא בוֹ טַעַם, אַף דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה כֵּן. וְכֵן מָצִינוּ בְמַסֶּכֶת חֲגִיגָה, שֶׁאָמַר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן־הוֹרְקְנוֹס דְּבָרִים שֶׁלֹּא שְׁמָעָתַן אֹזֶן, בְּמַעֲשֵׂה מֶרְכָּבָה:


**That which has been is what will be, etc.** In whatever he learns, in a matter that is an exchange for the sun, there is nothing new. He will see only that which already was, which was created in the six days of creation.³⁴ But one who engages in the study of Torah constantly finds new insights therein, as the matter is stated, “her breasts will satisfy you at all times.”³⁵ Just as this breast, whenever the infant feels it, he finds a taste in it, so are the words of Torah, and likewise we find in Maseches Chagigah,³⁶ that Rabbi Eliezer son of Horkenos said things that the ear had not heard, concerning the account of the “Celestial Chariot.”

footnotes:
³⁴ It is interesting to note that the Tiferes Yehonasan explains the verse dealing with the generation of separation in Bereishis 11:3, “ונלבנה לבנים [let us make bricks],” that the people attempted to build rocket ships [moon=לבנה] and venture into outer space.
³⁵ Mishlei 5:19.
³⁶ See Maseches Eiruvin 54b where the foregoing is discussed. However, the account of Rabbi Eliezer son of Horkenos does not appear in Maseches Chagigah.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:10:1)


**יֵשׁ דָּבָר.** בָּא לְיָדְךָ תַּחַת הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ שֶׁיֹּאמַר לְךָ הָאוֹמֵר עָלָיו: "רְאֵה זֶה, דָּבָר חָדָשׁ הוּא", וְאֵינוֹ חָדָשׁ, שֶׁכְּבָר הָיָה בָעוֹלָמִים שֶׁעָבְרוּ לְפָנֵינוּ, אֶלָּא שֶׁ"אֵין זִכְרוֹן לָרִאשֹׁנִים", לְכַךְ דּוֹמֶה לִהְיוֹת חָדָשׁ:


**Sometimes there is a thing.** That comes to your hand under the sun about which the speaker will tell you, “See, this is a new thing!” But it is not new, for it has already been so for ages that have passed before us, only “there is no remembrance of earlier generations.”³⁷ Therefore, it appears to be new.

footnotes:
³⁷ Below, verse 11.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:11:1)


**וְגַם לָאַחֲרֹנִים שֶׁיִּהְיוּ.** לְאַחֲרֵינוּ, לֹא יִהְיֶה לָהֶם זִכְרוֹן בַּדּוֹרוֹת שֶׁיִּהְיוּ לָאַחֲרוֹנָה לָהֶם. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה דוֹרְשׁוֹ כְנֶגֶד אִבּוּד זֵכֶר עֲמָלֵק, וְסוֹפוֹ לִמָּחוֹת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "וְלֹא־יִהְיֶה שָׂרִיד לְבֵית עֵשָׂו":


**Also of the later generations that are to come.** After us, will have no recollection in the generations that will be after them. And the Midrash Aggadah expounds this as referring to the obliteration of Amaleik’s remembrance, [whose remembrance] will ultimately be obliterated, as it is stated, “and the house of Eisav will have no survivor.”³⁸

footnotes:
³⁸ Ovadyah 1:18.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:12:1)


**אֲנִי קֹהֶלֶת הָיִיתִי מֶלֶךְ.** עַל־כָּל־הָעוֹלָם, וְלִבְסוֹף עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְלִבְסוֹף עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם לְבַדָּהּ, וְלִבְסוֹף עַל מַקְלִי, שֶׁהֲרֵי נֶאֱמַר: "הָיִיתִי מֶלֶךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָיִם". אֲבָל עַכְשָׁיו אֵינִי מֶלֶךְ:


**I, Koheles, have been king.** Over the whole world, and later, over Yisroel, and then, over Yerusholayim alone, and finally, over my staff,³⁹ for it says, “[I] have been king ... in Yerusholayim,” but now, I am no longer king.⁴⁰

footnotes:
³⁹ See Maseches Sanhedrin 20b.
⁴⁰ Koheles exemplifies the absurdity of being proud, regardless of one’s position in society. For time gives rise to many changes; Koheles went from reigning over the whole world to being dethroned.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:13:1)


**וְנָתַתִּי אֶת־לִבִּי לִדְרוֹשׁ.** בַּתּוֹרָה, הִיא הַחָכְמָה, וּלְהִתְבּוֹנֵן בָּהּ עַל כָּל־הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הָרַע הָאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה הַנַּעֲשֶׂה תַּחַת הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ, וּבִינוֹתִי בָהּ שֶׁהוּא הָעִנְיָן הָרָע אֲשֶׁר נָתַן הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לִפְנֵי בְנֵי הָאָדָם "אֶת־הַחַיִים וְאֶת־הַטּוֹב וְאֶת הָמָּוֶת וְאֶת־הָרָע":


**And I applied my heart to search.** In the Torah, which is wisdom, and to ponder over it concerning all the evil deeds mentioned above, which are committed under the sun, and I pondered over it that it is a sore task that the Holy One, Blessed Is He, set before mankind. “The life and the good, and the death and the evil.”⁴¹

footnotes:
⁴¹ Devarim 30:15.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:13:2)


**עִנְיָן רָע.** מִנְהָג רַע לָהֶם:


**An evil thing.** They have evil behavior.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:13:3)


**לַעֲנוֹת בּוֹ.** לְהִתְנַהֵג בּוֹ. "עִנְיָן" יֵשׁ לְפָתְרוֹ לְשׁוֹן מָעוֹן וְדִירָה, וְיֵשׁ לְפָתְרוֹ לְשׁוֹן עִיּוּן וּמַחֲשָׁבָה, וְכֵן "לַעֲנוֹת בּוֹ":


**To be afflicted with.** To behave with it. “עִנְיָן” may be interpreted as an expression of an abode [=מָעוֹן and a dwelling, and it may also be interpreted as an expression of study [=עִיּוּן and thought, and the same applies [for the interpretation of] ‘לַעֲנוֹת בּוֹ.’⁴²

footnotes:
⁴² Alternatively ‘לענות בו’ means “to be afflicted with.” (Metsudas Tzion)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:13:4)


**נָתַן אֱלֹהִים.** הֵנִיחַ לִפְנֵיהֶם:


**God has given.** Has placed before them.⁴³

footnotes:
⁴³ And they have been given the freedom to choose [=בחירה].



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:14:1)


**וּרְעוּת רוּחַ.** שֶׁבֶר רוּחַ, כְּמוֹ "רֹעוּ עַמִּים וָחֹתּוּ". רוּחַ טלנ"ט. סוֹף הַמַּעֲשֶׂה בָא לִידֵי כְאֵב לֵב:


**And vexation of spirit.** The breaking [=וּרְעוּת of the spirit,⁴⁴ as in, “Shatter רֹעוּ, O peoples, and be devastated.”⁴⁵ “רוּחַ” is *talent* in O.F. At the end of the deed it becomes heartache.

footnotes:
⁴⁴ Alternatively, ורעות רוח means, “and a grasping [from רעה] after wind,” i.e., man’s effort is as hopeless as trying to catch the wind.
⁴⁵ Yeshayahu 8:9.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:15:1)


**מְעֻוָּת.** בְּחַיָּיו לֹא יוּכַל לִתְקֹן מִשֶּׁמֵּת. מִי שֶׁטָּרַח בְּעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת יֹאכַל בְּשַׁבָּת. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵרְשׁוּ עַל הַבָּא עַל הָעֶרְוָה וְהוֹלִיד מַמְזֵר, אוֹ עַל תַּלְמִיד חָכָם הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִן הַתּוֹרָה, שֶׁהָיָה יָשָׁר מִתְּחִלָּתֹוֹ וְנִתְעַוֵּת:


**That which is crooked.** During his lifetime, will not be able to be straightened after he dies. Whoever toiled on the eve of Shabbos will eat on Shabbos.⁴⁶ And our Sages explained this as referring to one who had illicit relations with a woman and begot a *mamzer*, or to a Torah scholar who abandoned the Torah, [i.e.,] he was originally straight, and became crooked.⁴⁷ or *Tefillah* in their respective required time periods; in Maseches Berachos 26a.

footnotes:
⁴⁶ See Maseches Avodah Zarah 3a.
⁴⁷ See Mishnayos Chagigah 1:7. Another application of this verse is in reference to a person who failed to recite Shema


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:15:2)


**וְחֶסְרוֹן לֹא־יוּכַל לְהִמָּנוֹת.** זֶה שֶׁחִסֵּר עַצְמוֹ מִמִּנְיַן הַכְּשֵׁרִים, לֹא יוּכַל לְהִמָּנוֹת עִמָּהֶם בְּקִבּוּל שְׂכָרָם:


**And that which is wanting cannot be counted.** The one who excluded himself from the group,⁴⁸ cannot be counted with them to share their reward.

footnotes:
⁴⁸ I.e., that was formed to perform a mitzvah; see Maseches Berachos 26a.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:16:1)


**דִּבַּרְתִּי אֲנִי עִם־לִבִּי.** עַכְשָׂיו שֶׁיָּרַדְתִּי מִגְּדֻלָּתִי, אֲנִי נוֹתֵן לִבִּי לֵאמֹר: "מִי יֹאמַר עָלַי שֶׁאָבֹא לִידֵי מִדָּה זוּ"?: **אֲנִי הִנֵּה הִגְדַּלְתִּי וְגוֹ'**:


**I said to myself.** Now that I have sunk from my greatness, I speak to myself, saying, “Who would have said about me that I would come to such a state?” **Behold, I have grown, etc.**



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:17:1)


**וָאֶתְּנָה** עַכְשָׁיו אֶת־לִבִּי לָדַעַת אֶת־טִיב הַחָכְמָה מַה סּוֹפָהּ, וְאֶת־טִיב הַהוֹלֵלוֹת וְהַסִּכְלוּת:


**And I applied** my heart, now, to know the nature of wisdom, what its end is, and the nature of madness and folly.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:17:2)


**הֹלֵלוֹת.** שִׁעֲמוּם וְטֵרוּף הַדַּעַת, לְשׁוֹן עִרְבּוּב, כְּמוֹ "מָהוּל בַּמָּיִם":


**Madness.** Dullness and confusion of thoughts, an expression of mixing, as in, “diluted מָהוּל with water.”⁴⁹

footnotes:
⁴⁹ Yeshayahu 1:22.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:17:3)


**וְשִׂכְלוּת.** שְׁטוּת:


**And folly.** Foolishness.⁵⁰

footnotes:
⁵⁰ שׂכלות has the same meaning as if it were spelled  סכלות [=foolishness], as in, “and to grasp foolishness [סכלות]” in 2:3 and as in, “and madness and foolishness [סכלות]” in 2:12 below.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 1:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/1:17:4)


**יָדַעְתִּי** עַתָּה, שֶׁגַּם הַחָכְמָה יֵשׁ בּוֹ שֶׁבֶר רוּחַ, כִּי בְּרֹב הַחָכְמָה, אָדָם סוֹמֵךְ עַל רֹב חָכְמָתוֹ, וְאֵינוֹ מִתְרַחֵק מִן הָאִסּוּר, וּבָא רָב־כָּעַס לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא. אֲנִי אָמַרְתִּי: "אַרְבֶּה סוּסִים, וְלֹא אָשִׁיב אֶת־הָעָם מִצְרָיְמָה", וּבַסּוֹף הֲשִׁיבוֹתִי. אֲנִי אָמַרְתִּי: "אַרְבֶּה נָשִׁים וְלֹא יָסוּר לְבָבִי", וַהֲרֵי נִכְתַּב עָלַי "נָשָׁיו הִטּוּ אֶת־לְבָבוֹ". וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר שֶׁעַל רֹב חָכְמָתוֹ הוּא סָמַךְ, וְעָשָׂה כַמָּה דְבָרִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר "נְאֻם הַגֶּבֶר לְאִיתִיאֵל לְאִיתִיאֵל וְאֻכָל":


**I understand** now, that also wisdom has frustration in it, for in great wisdom, a person relies on his great wisdom and does not distance himself from the prohibition, and much vexation comes to the Holy One, Blessed Is He. I said, “I will get many horses, but I will not return the people to Egypt,” but ultimately, I returned. I said, “I will take many wives, but they will not turn my heart away,” but it is written about me, “his wives swayed his heart.”⁵¹ And similarly it states that he relied on his great wisdom and did many things, as it is stated, “the words of this man to Isieil, because God is with me, I will be able.”⁵²

footnotes:
⁵¹ I Melachim 11:4.
⁵² Mishlei 30:1.



## Chapter 2


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:1:1)


**אָמַרְתִּי אֲנִי בְּלִבִּי.** הוֹאִיל וְכֵן הוּא, אֶחְדַל לִי מִן הַחָכְמָה, וְאֶעֱסֹק בְּמִשְׁתֶּה תָמִיד:


**I said to myself.** Since that is so, I will refrain from wisdom, and I will indulge in drinking.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:1:2)


**אַנַסְּכָה.** לְשׁוֹן מֶסֶךְ יַיִן לִשְׁתּוֹת, כְּמוֹ, "מָסְכָה יֵינָהּ". עֵרוּב יַיִן בְּמַיִם לְתַקְּנוֹ אוֹ עֵרוּב בְּשָׂמִים בְּיַיִן לְקוֹנְדִיטוֹן:


**I will try.** אַנַסְּכָה is an expression of mixing wine [=מֶסֶךְ to drink,¹ as in, “she mixed her wine,”² the mixing of wine with water to improve it or the mixing of spices with wine for create a spiced wine.

footnotes:
¹ Alternatively, אנסכה means “I will test [=נסה].” (Metsudas Tzion); or “I will govern [=נסיך].” (Sforno)
² Mishlei 9:2.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:1:3)


**וּרְאֵה בְטוֹב.** כְּמוֹ "וּרְאוֹת בְּטוֹב":


**And enjoy the good life.** וּרְאֵה is the same as וּרְאוֹת [=to enjoy].³)

footnotes:
³ I.e., וראה is not a command. (Sifsei Chachomim


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:1:4)


**וְהִנֵּה גַם־הוּא הָבֶל.** שֶׁהֲרֵי רָאִיתִי בִנְבוּאָה שֶׁהַרְבֵּה קִלְקוּלִים בָּאִים מִתּוֹךְ שְׂחוֹק. בֵּלְשַׁצַּר מֵת מִתּוֹךְ מִשְׁתֶּה, אַנְשֵׁי דוֹר הַמַּבּוּל נִשְׁטְפוּ מִתּוֹךְ רֹב טוֹבָה שֶׁהִשְׁפַּעְתָּ לָהֶם:


**And behold, this too was vanity.** For I saw prophetically that much evil is caused by lighthearted frivolity, [e.g.,] Belshatzar died as a result of a banquet, [and] the people of the generation of the flood were inundated because of the abundant goodness that You bestowed upon them.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:2:1)


**לִשְׂחוֹק אָמַרְתִּי מְהוֹלָל.** מְעֹרֶבֶת בִּבְכִי וַאֲנָחָה:


**Of laughter I said it is senseless.** Mixed with cries and sighs.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Alternatively, ‘מהולל’ means praiseworthy [=הלל], referring to the joy and delight awaiting the righteous in the World to Come, which is indeed praiseworthy. See Maseches Shabbos 30b.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:2:2)


**וּלְשִׂמְחָה מַה־זֹה.** טוֹבָה עֹשָׂה? הֲרֵי סוֹפָהּ תּוּגָה:


**And of joy, what does it [accomplish]?** [What] good is it if it ends in grief.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:3:1)


**תַּרְתִּי בְלִבִּי.** חָזַרְתִּי לָתוּר בְּלִבִּי לְהַחֲזִיק בְּכֻלָּן, בְּמִשְׁתֶּה, בְּחָכְמָה, וּבְסִכְלוּת, וְלִמְשֹׁךְ וּלְעַדֵּן בְּמִשְׁתֵּה הַיַּיִן אֶת־בְּשָׂרִי. כָּל־סְעֻדַּת עֹנֶג קְרוּיָה עַל שֵׁם הַיָּיִן:


**I searched in my heart.** I repeatedly searched in my heart to grasp them all, partying, wisdom, and folly, and to stimulate and to pamper my body by imbibing in wine. Every luxurious banquet is referred to as wine [=יַיִּן.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:3:2)


**וְלִבִּי נֹהֵג בַּחָכְמָה.** אַף אִם בְּשָׂרִי נִמְשָׁךְ בַּיַּיִן, לִבִּי מִתְגַּלְגַּל בַּחָכְמָה, לְהַחֲזִיק בַּתּוֹרָה:


**And my heart conducted itself with wisdom.** Even if my body is imbibed with wine, my heart conducted itself with wisdom and Torah observance.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:3:3)


**וְלֶאֱחֹז בְּסִכְלוּת.** בִּדְבָרִים הַדּוֹמִים לִי לְסִכְלוּת, שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי עֲלֵיהֶם, "לְאִיתִיאֵל וְאֻכָל", וּכְגוֹן לְבִישַׁת שַׁעַטְנֵז וְכִלְאֵי הַכֶּרֶם, שֶׁהַשָּׂטָן וְאֻמּוֹת הָעוֹלָם מְשִׁיבִין עֲלֵיהֶם, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר, "טוֹב אֲשֶׁר תֶּאֱחֹז בָּזֶה". וְגַם עַל שָׁאוּל שֶׁנִּדְמָה בְעֵינָיו סִכְלוּת לַהֲרוֹג מֵאִישׁ וְעַד אִשָּׁה, מֵעוֹלֵל וְעַד יוֹנֵק, וְהִיא מִצְוַת הַמָּקוֹם, וְקוֹרֵא אוֹתָהּ סִכְלוּת:


**And to grasp foolishness.** To things that appear foolish to me, concerning which I said, “Because God is with me, I will be able,”⁵ for example, the [prohibition against] wearing sha’atnez and having mingled species in a vineyard, which Satan and the nations of the world are critical of, and so it states, “It is best to take hold of one,”⁶ and also, regarding Shaul, in whose eyes it seemed foolish to kill both man and woman, infant and suckling, but it was the commandment of the Omnipresent, and he called it foolishness!

footnotes:
⁵ Mishlei 30:1.
⁶ Below 7:18.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:4:1)


**הִגְדַּלְתִּי מַעֲשָׂי.** בִּימֵי גְדֻלָּתִי:


**I made great works.** In the days of my greatness.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:5:1)


**עֵץ כָּל־פֶּרִי.** שֶׁהָיָה שְׁלֹמֹה מַכִּיר בְּחָכְמָתוֹ אֶת־גִּידֵי הָאָרֶץ, אֵיזֶה גִיד הוֹלֵךְ אֶל כּוּשׁ וְנָטַע בּוֹ פִּלְפֻּלִין, אֵיזֶה הוֹלֵךְ לְאֶרֶץ חֲרוּבִין וְנָטַע בּוֹ חֲרוּבִין, שֶׁכָּל־גִּידֵי הָאֲרָצוֹת בָּאִים לְצִיּוֹן, שֶׁמִּשָּׁם מַשְׁתִּיתוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "מִצִּיּוֹן מִכְלַל־יֹפִי". לְכַךְ נֶאֱמַר: "עֵץ כָּל־פֶּרִי", בְּמִדְרַשׁ תַּנְחוּמָא:


**Every kind of fruit tree.** For Shlomo with his wisdom recognized the veins of the earth, which vein goes to Cush, and there he planted peppers [which normally does not grow in Eretz Yisroel]; which one goes to a land of carobs, and there he planted carob trees. For all the veins of the lands come to Tzion, from where the world was founded, as it is stated, “Out of Tzion, the perfect beauty.”⁷ Therefore, it is stated, “every kind of fruit tree.” [This is found] in Midrash Tanchuma.⁸

footnotes:
⁷ Tehillim 50:2.
⁸ The Gemara in Maseches Bava Kamma 82b states that it is forbidden to plant gardens and orchards in Yerusholayim. Apparently that is what prompted the Targum for this verse to describe that the boundaries for these plants and orchards were from “the wall of Yerusholayim until the banks of the water of Shiloach.” (Gishmei Bracha)



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:6:1)


**בְּרֵכוֹת מָיִם.** כְּמִין בִּיבָרִים שֶׁל דָּגִים שֶׁחוֹפְרִין בְּקַרְקַע:


**Pools of water.** בְּרֵכוֹת is] similar to ponds for fish, which are dug into the ground.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:8:1)


**וּסְגֻלַּת מְלָכִים.** גִּנְזֵי מְלָכִים, זָהָב וְכֶסֶף וְאֶבֶן יְקָרָה שֶׁהַמְּלָכִים מְסַגְּלִים בְּגִנְזֵיהֶם:


**And treasures of kings.** The treasures of the kings, gold, silver and precious stones, which the kings collect in their treasure houses.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:8:2)


**וְהַמְּדִינוֹת.** סְגֻלַּת כָּל־סוֹחֲרִים:


**And provinces.** The treasure of all merchants.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:8:3)


**שָׁרִים וְשָׁרוֹת.** מִינֵי כְלֵי זֶמֶר:


**Singers and musical instruments.** Various types of musical instruments.⁹

footnotes:
⁹ See Maseches Gittin 68a. According to Rashi, שׁרים ושׁרות are various types of musical instruments. Alternatively, שׁרים ושׁרות are male singers and female singers respectively.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:8:4)


**שִׁדָּה וְשִׁדּוֹת.** מַרְכְּבוֹת נוֹי, עֶגְלוֹת צָב, וּבִלְשׁוֹן גְּמָרָא יֵשׁ: שִׁדָּה, תֵּיבָה וּמִגְדָּל:


**Chests and chests of them.** Beautiful coaches, covered wagons, a term used in the Gemara, “a coach שִׁדָּה, a chest and a closet.”¹⁰ ¹¹

footnotes:
¹⁰ Mishnayos Keilim 18:3.
¹¹ Alternatively, שׁדה ושׁדות refers to a harem, i.e., women taken forcefully [שדד=plunder]. (Ibn Ezra)



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:9:1)


**אַף חָכְמָתִי.** גַּם חָכְמָתִי לֹא הִנַּחְתִּי בִשְׁבִיל כָּל־הַמַּעֲשִׂים הָאֵלֶּה, וְעָמְדָה לִי וְלֹא שְׁכַחְתִּיהָ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: עָמְדָה לִי לְעֶזְרָה מִכָּל־אֵלֶּה:


**Also my wisdom.** Also,¹² I did not forsake my wisdom because of all these affairs, and it remained with me and I did not forget it. Another explanation, it stood by me to aid me against all these.

footnotes:
¹² Alternatively, אף means “anger” and the verse is translated as, “only the [Torah] wisdom that I acquired through exertion [אף] is what remained with me.” The Gemara in Maseches Berachos 63b and in Maseches Eiruvin 54a indicates that the study of Torah during trying times is most commendable and will result in great success and will not soon be forgotten. Or, unlike some preachers who do not practice what they preach, I, says Koheles, internalized and conducted my life [עמדה לי] according to what I preached. (Yismach Moshe)



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:10:1)


**לֹא אָצַלְתִּי.** לֹא רִחַקְתִּי לְהַבְדִּיל מֵהֶם, וְכֵן, "וַיָּאצֶל מִן־הָרוּחַ. . . "וַיִּתֵּן עַל . . . הַזְּקֵנִים", כִּמְנוֹרָה שֶׁמַּדְלִיקִין מִמֶּנָּה נֵרוֹת הַרְבֵּה וְאֵין אוֹרָהּ חָסֵר כְּלוּם:


**I did not deny.** I did not distance myself to separate from them, and similarly, “and He held back some of the spirit [that was upon him] and he gave it unto ... the elders,”¹³ like a candelabrum from which many candles are kindled, and none of its light is diminished.

footnotes:
¹³ Bamidbar 11:25.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:10:2)


**וְזֶה־הָיָה חֶלְקִי.** וְאַחֲרֵי עֲשׂוֹתִי כָל־אֵלֶּה, אֵין לִי מִכֻּלָּם אֶלָּא זֶה. רַב וּשְׁמוּאֵל, חַד אָמַר מַקְלוֹ, וְחַד אָמַר קִידוֹ. מַקֵּידָה שֶׁל חֶרֶס שֶׁשּׁוֹתִין בָּה, וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים בְּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה כָּל־הָעִנְיָן בְּבָתֵּי מִדְרָשׁוֹת וְתַלְמִידִים וּבְבָתֵּי כְנֵסִיּוֹת:


**And this was my portion [reward].** And after doing all these, I have nothing more than this. Rav and Shmuel, one says, his staff, and one says, his cup.¹⁴ מַקֵּידָה is an earthenware cup, from which people drink.¹⁵ Others in the Midrash Aggadah interpret the entire section as referring to study halls, students, and synagogues.

footnotes:
¹⁴ See Maseches Sanhedrin 20b. The Gemara in Maseches Eiruvin 21b states that Shlomo established eiruvei chatzeiros [a procedure which allows carrying in courtyards on Shabbos] and netilas yodayim [ritual washing of one’s hands before eating]. Accordingly, מקלו [=his staff] alludes to eiruvei chatzeiros and קידו [=cup] alludes to netilas yodayim. (R’ Eliyahu Greiditzer)
¹⁵ See Rashi in Maseches Sanhedrin 20b.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:10:3)


**יַעַר צוֹמֵחַ עֵצִים.** עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ לִמְלֶאכֶת שָׂדוֹת וּכְרָמִים:


**The forest growing with trees.** The ignorant people, for working in fields and vineyards.¹⁶)

footnotes:
¹⁶ This Rashi on verse 6 appears to be out of place. Some texts treat the caption as part of Rashi narrative, i.e., that “the Midrash Aggadah interprets the entire section ... and synagogues, and the forest growing with trees [refers to] the ignorant people, etc.” (Sifsei Chachomim



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:11:1)


**וּפָנִיתִי.** עַתָּה בְּכָל־מַעֲשַׂי, וְרוֹאֶה אֲנִי שֶׁאֵין יִתְרוֹן בָּהֶם, כִּי מִכֻּלָּם אֲנִי חָסֵר:


**Then I looked.** Now at all my deeds, and I see that there is no benefit in them, for from all of them I am lacking.¹⁷

footnotes:
¹⁷ I.e., they do not yield the eternal benefit I had expected of them.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:12:1)


**וּפָנִיתִי אֲנִי לִרְאוֹת חָכְמָה.** פּוֹנֶה אֲנִי מִכָּל־עֲסָקַי לְהִתְבּוֹנֵן בַּתּוֹרָה וְהוֹלֵלוֹת וְסִכְלוּת, עֹנֶשׁ עֲבֵרוֹת:


**I [then] turned my attention to behold wisdom.** I turn away from all my affairs to delve into Torah, and madness and folly, [i.e.,] the punishment for transgressions.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:12:2)


**כִּי מֶה הָאָדָם שֶׁיָּבוֹא אַחֲרֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ.** לְהִתְחַנֵּן לוֹ עַל גְּזֵרָה שֶׁגָּזְרוּ עָלָיו וּכְבָר עֲשָׂאוּהוּ לְמַעֲשֵׂה הַגְּזֵרָה לְגָזְרָה, טוֹב לוֹ לְהִתְבּוֹנֵן תְּחִלָּה בְמַעֲשָׂיו וְלֹא יִצְטָרֵךְ לְבַקֵּשׁ:


**For what value is the man who would come after the king.** To supplicate him concerning a decree that they decreed upon him, and they already executed the decree and it is already in effect. It is better for him to consider at first his actions, and he would not find it necessary for him to appeal.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:13:1)


**מִן־הַסִּכְלוּת.** הוּא הָרֶשַׁע:


**Over foolishness.** That is wickedness.¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁸ The analogy of wisdom and foolishness to light [אור] and darkness [חשך] can be explained that אור is an all inclusive term which can mean day or night, but חשך refers to night only. See Maseches Pesachim 2a-b. Similarly, a wise person, when the situation calls for it, can act in a foolish manner [as Dovid did when he escaped from Avimelech], whereas a foolish person cannot act as a wise person. (R. Leibish Charif)



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:14:1)


**הֶחָכָם עֵינָיו בְּרֹאשׁוֹ.** בִּתְחִלַּת הַדָּבָר, מִסְתַּכֵּל מַה־יְהֵא בְסוֹפָהּ:


**The wise man, his eyes are in his head.** In the beginning [=בְּרֹאשׁוֹ of the matter, he contemplates the end results.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ Note the similarity to “Who is wise? He who visualizes what the future holds,” in Maseches Tamid 32a.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:14:2)


**וְיָדַעְתִּי גַם־אָנִי.** גַּם אֲנִי, אֲשֶׁר מְשַׁבֵּחַ אֶת־הֶחָכָם מִן הַכְּסִיל, יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם יָמוּתוּ:


**But I also understood.** Also, I [know], who praise the wise man over the fool, I know that they both will die.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:15:1)


**וְאָמַרְתִּי . . . בְּלִבִּי וְגוֹ'.** כְּלוֹמַר: לְפִי שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם מֵתִים, שֶׁמָּא אֲהַרְהֵר בְּלִבִּי מֵעַתָּה, כְּמִקְרֵה הָרָשָׁע גַּם אֲנִי יִקְרֵנֵי, וְלָמָּה אֶהְיֶה צַדִּיק אָז יוֹתֵר?:


**And I said to myself, etc.** I.e., since they will both die, perhaps I will think in my heart from now on that as it happens to the wicked man, so will it happen to me, so why should I be more righteous?


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:15:2)


**וְדִבַּרְתִּי בְלִבִּי.** שֶׁאִם אֲהַרְהֵר כֵּן, הֶבֶל הוּא, כִּי אֵין זִכְרוֹן הֶחָכָם וְהַכְּסִיל שָׁוִין; אַחֲרֵי מוֹתָן לֹא יִזָּכְרוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם יַחַד, שֶׁזֶּה זִכְרוֹנוֹ לְטוֹבָה, וְזֶה זִכְרוֹנוֹ לְרָעָה:


**Then I said to myself.** That if I will think so, that is vanity, for the remembrances of the wise man and the fool are not equal. After their deaths, both of them will not be remembered together, for this one will be remembered for good, and this one will be remembered for evil.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:16:1)


**בְּשֶׁכְּבָר הַיָּמִים הַבָּאִים הַכֹּל נִשְׁכָּח.** בִּשְׁבִיל אֲשֶׁר אֲנִי רוֹאֶה אֶת־הָרְשָׁעִים אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ כְבָר וְהִצְלִיחוּ מְאֹד, וּבַיָּמִים הַבָּאִים אַחֲרֵיהֶם נִשְׁכְּחָה כָל־גְּבוּרָתָם וְהַצְלָחָתָם:


**Since in the days to come all will be forgotten.** Because I see the wicked who already lived, and who were very successful, and in the days that came after them, all their power and their success were forgotten.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:16:2)


**וְאֵיךְ יָמוּת הֶחָכָם עִם־הַכְּסִיל.** אֲנִי רוֹאֶה הַצַּדִּיקִים מַצְלִיחִים בְּמִיתָתָם וּמוֹעִילִים לִבְנֵיהֶם, כְּגוֹן, "וְזָכַרְתִּי אֶת־בְּרִיתִי יַעֲקוֹב וְגוֹ'"; "זָכַרְתִּי לָךְ חֶסֶד נְעוּרַיִךְ":


**And how can the death of the wise man be [like the death] of the fool.** I observe the righteous succeeding in their deaths and availing their children, for example, “And I will remember My covenant with Yaakov, etc.,”²⁰ [and] “I remember for you the kindness of your youth.”²¹

footnotes:
²⁰ Vayikra 26:42.
²¹ Yirmiyahu 2:2.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:17:1)


**וְשָׂנֵאתִי אֶת־הַחַיִּים.** שֶׁהָיָה מִתְנַבֵּא עַל דּוֹרוֹ שֶׁל רְחַבְעָם שֶׁהָיוּ רְשָׁעִים:


**And [so] I hated life.** He was prophesying about the generation of Rechovam, who were wicked [people].²²

footnotes:
²² During Rechovam’s reign, ten tribes defected and joined forces with him.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:19:1)


**גַּם־זֶה הָבֶל.** גַּם זֶה אֶחָד מִן הַהֲבָלִים שֶׁנִּבְרְאוּ בָעוֹלָם, שֶׁהֶחָכָם יָגֵעַ וְהַכְּסִיל יוֹרְשׁוֹ:


**This too is vanity.** This too is one of the vanities that were created in the world, that the wise man labors, and the fool inherits him.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:20:1)


**וְסַבּוֹתִי אֲנִי לְיַאֵשׁ.** שֶׁלֹּא לִיגַע וְלַעֲמֹל:


**And [so] I turned my heart to despair.** Neither to toil nor to labor.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:21:1)


**כִּי־יֵשׁ אָדָם.** מַשְׁמָעוֹ כִפְשׁוּטוֹ. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה בְתַנְחוּמָא מְכַנֵּהוּ כְּלַפֵּי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ, "וְעַל־דְּמוּת הַכִּסֵּא דְּמוּת כְּמַרְאֵה אָדָם":


**For there is a man.** Its interpretation is according to its plain meaning. But the Midrash Aggadah in Tanchuma interprets it as an allegorical reference to the Holy One, Blessed Is He, concerning Whom it is stated, “and upon the likeness of the throne there was a likeness like the appearance of a man.”²³

footnotes:
²³ Yechezkeil 1:26.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:21:2)


**שֶׁעֲמָלוֹ בְּחָכְמָה.** שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "ה' בְּחָכְמָה יָסַד אָרֶץ . . . בְּדַעְתּוֹ תְּהוֹמוֹת נִבְקָעוּ", וְלַבְּרִיּוֹת שֶׁלֹּא עָמְלוּ בוֹ נָתַן חֵלֶק בּוֹ:


**Who labored with wisdom.** As it is stated, “Adonoy founded the earth with wisdom ... with His knowledge the depths were split,”²⁴ and to the creatures who had not labored in it, He left a share in it.²⁵

footnotes:
²⁴ Mishlei 3:19-20.
²⁵ This can be referring to a situation where one publicly shamed his friend, that according to our Sages his friend receives his share in Gan Eiden. (Kehilas Yaakov). Or, as the Gemara Maseches Chagigah 15a states that God created for every person a portion of Gan Eiden and a portion of Gehinnom, and when a wicked person fails to earn his portion in Gan Eiden, it is given to a righteous person in addition to his own portion that he earned.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:21:3)


**גַּם־זֶה הֶבֶל וְרָעָה רַבָּה.** וְהֵם נַעֲשׂוּ דוֹר שֶׁל הֶבֶל, וְרַבָּה רָעַת הָאָדָם בָּאָרֶץ בְּדוֹר הַמַּבּוּל:


**This too is vanity and a great evil.** And they became a generation of vanity and “the wickedness of man was great upon the earth,”²⁶ in the generation of the flood.

footnotes:
²⁶ Bereishis 6:5. The text in Bereishis which is referring to the generation of the flood, states “כי רבה רעת האדם” which is similar to the wording of this verse “רבה רעה” [and a great evil].



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:22:1)


**כִּי מֶה־הֹוֶה וְגוֹ'.** כִּי מַה מּוֹתָר הֹוֶה לָאָדָם בְּכָל־עֲמָלוֹ וְשִׁבְרוֹן לִבּוֹ בְעָמָל וּדְאָגָה שֶׁהוּא עָמֵל וּמֵנִיחַ לַאֲחֵרִים:


**For what has, etc.** For what advantage does man gain from all his toil and frustration he endures with [his] labor and worry, which he toils, and ultimately leaving [his possessions] to others.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:23:1)


**עִנְיָנוֹ.** מִנְהָגוֹ:


**His lot.** His custom.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:23:2)


**גַּם־זֶה.** אֶחָד מִן הַהֲבָלִים הַנּוֹהֲגִים בָּעוֹלָם:


**This too.** Is one of the vanities that prevail in the world.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:24:1)


**אֵין טוֹב בָּאָדָם.** בִּתְמִיהַּ, שֶׁיֹּאכַל וְיִשְׁתֶּה וְיַרְאֶה אֶת־נַפְשׁוֹ טוֹב, כְּלוֹמַר, יִתֵּן לִבּוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת מִשְׁפָּט וּצְדָקָה עִם הַמַּאֲכָל וְהַמִּשְׁתֶּה, וְכֵן נֶאֱמַר לִיהוֹיָקִים, "אָבִיךָ הֲלוֹא אָכַל וְשָׁתָה וְעָשָׂה מִשְׁפָּט וּצְדָקָה אָז טוֹב לוֹ:


**Is it not beneficial for man.** This is a question, “that he eat and drink and shows himself content?” i.e., “he guides his heart to perform justice and righteousness with the food and drink, and so it was stated to Yehoyakim, “Did not your father eat and drink and practice justice and righteousness and then it was well with him?”²⁷

footnotes:
²⁷ Yirmiyahu 22:15.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:25:1)


**כִּי מִי יֹאכַל וְגוֹ'.** לָמָּה לֹא אֶשְׂמַח בְּחֶלְקִי, בְּמַאֲכָל וּבְמִשְׁתֶּה? מִי רָאוּי לֶאֱכֹל אֶת־יְגִיעִי, וּמִי יְמַהֵר לְבָלְעָהּ מִבַּלְעָדָי?:


**For who should eat, etc.** Why should I not rejoice with my portion in food and drink? Who deserves to eat what I toiled for, and who will hurry to swallow it, except me?


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:25:2)


**חוּץ מִמֶּנִּי.** מִבַּלְעָדָי. זוּ מִדַּת הָרְשָׁעִים הִיא, שֶׁאוֹסְפִים לְצֹרֶךְ הָאֲחֵרִים:


**If not I.** Except me. This is the trait of the wicked, who hoard [their possessions] for others.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:26:1)


**כִּי לְאָדָם שֶׁטּוֹב לְפָנָיו.** לִפְנֵי הָאֱלֹהִים הַנִּזְכָּר לְמַעְלָה, "כִּי מִיַּד הָאֱלֹהִים הִיא":


**For to the man who is good in His sight.** Before God, mentioned above, “that it is from the hand of God.”²⁸

footnotes:
²⁸ Above, verse 24.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:26:2)


**נָתַן חָכְמָה וְדַעַת וְשִׂמְחָה.** לֵב לַעֲסֹק בַּתּוֹרָה וּבְמִצְוֹת וְלִשְׂמֹחַ בְּחֶלְקוֹ, בְּמַאֲכָל וּבְמִשְׁתֶּה וּכְסוּת נְקִיָּה:


**He gives wisdom, knowledge and joy.** A heart [i.e., intellect] to engage in Torah [study] and in [fulfilling] the commandments and to rejoice in his portion of eating, drinking, and [donning] clean clothing.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:26:3)


**וְלַחוֹטֶא נָתַן עִנְיָן.** מִנְהָג וּדְאָגָה לֶאֱסֹף וְלִכְנוֹס וְלָתֵת לְטוֹב לִפְנֵי הָאֱלֹהִים, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "וַתָּשֶׂם אֶסְתֵּר אֶת־מָרְדְּכַי עַל־בֵּית הָמָן":


**But to the sinner He gives the urge.** A habit and concern to hoard and gather, and to give to him who is good before God, as the matter is stated, “and Esther put Mordechai in charge of Haman’s estate.”²⁹

footnotes:
²⁹ Esther 8:2.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 2:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/2:26:4)


**גַּם־זֶה.** אֶחָד מִן הַהֲבָלִים שֶׁנִּתְּנוּ לַבְּרִיּוֹת, שֶׁהֵם עֲמֵלִים וְאַחֵר נוֹטֵל:


**This too.** Is one of the vanities that were given to the creatures, that they labor, and someone else takes.



## Chapter 3


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:1:1)


**לַכֹּל זְמָן.** אַל יִשְׂמַח הָאוֹסֵף בְּהוֹן מֵהֶבֶל, כִּי אִם עַכְשָׁיו הוּא בְיָדוֹ, עוֹד יִירָשׁוּהוּ צַדִּיקִים, אֶלָּא שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא הִגִּיעַ הַזְּמַן, כִּי לְכָל־דָּבָר יֵשׁ זְמַן קָבוּעַ מָתַי יִהְיֶה:


**For everything there is a season.** Let not one who accumulates possessions out of vanity rejoice, for although he possesses them now, they will ultimately pass on to the righteous, only the time has not yet arrived, for everything has a set time¹ when it will be.²

footnotes:
¹ The word עת [=time] is repeated 29 times in this section, corresponding to the number of days in a lunar month.
² I.e., nothing is coincidental, rather there is a specific time for everything (Metsudas Dovid). Alternatively, one should not say that he has no time for Torah study or the performance of Mitzvos, because in reality לכל זמן, there is time for everything when, ועת לכל חפץ, if it is for an activity that he really desires [=חפץ], there is always time. (Kehilas Yaakov)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:1:2)


**לְכָל־חֵפֶץ.** לְכָל־דָּבָר. כָּל־הַדְּבָרִים קְרוּיִם חֲפָצִים בִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה:


**For everything.** For every thing [=חֵפֶץ. All things דְּבָרִים are called “חֲפָצִים” in the language of the Mishnah.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:2:1)


**עֵת לָלֶדֶת.** לְתִשְׁעָה חֳדָשִׁים:


**A time to be born.** At nine months.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:2:2)


**וְעֵת לָמוּת.** קֶצֶב שָׁנוֹת שֶׁל כָּל־דוֹר וָדוֹר:


**And a time to die.** The limit of the years of each generation.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:2:3)


**עֵת לָטַעַת.** גּוֹי וּמַמְלָכָה:


**A time to plant.** A nation and a kingdom.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:2:4)


**וְעֵת לַעֲקוֹר.** עֵת יָבֹא לְהֵעָקֵר:


**And a time to uproot.** A time will come for it to be uprooted.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:3:1)


**עֵת לַהֲרוֹג.** אוּמָּה שְׁלֵמָה, כְּשֶׁמַּגִיעַ יוֹם פְּקֻדָּתָהּ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, וּשְׁאֵרִיתֵךְ יַהֲרֹג:


**A time to kill.** An entire nation, when the day of its visitation comes, as it is stated, “and he will slay your remnant [with the sword].”³

footnotes:
³ Yeshayahu 14:30.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:3:2)


**וְעֵת לִרְפּוֹא.** שִׁבְרָם, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּמִצְרַיִם, "וְשָׁבוּ עַד־ה' וְנֶעְתַּר לָהֶם וּרְפָאָם":


**And a time to heal.** Their ruin, as the matter is stated concerning Egypt, “and they will repent to Adonoy, and He will accept their prayer and heal them.”⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Yeshayahu 19:22.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:3:3)


**עֵת לִפְרוֹץ.** חוֹמַת הָעִיר כְּשֶׁנִּגְזַר עָלֶיהָ, שֶׁנֶאֱמַר, וְחוֹמַת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם מְפֹרָצֶת:


**A time to wreck.** The wall of the city, when it is decreed upon it, as it is stated, “and the wall of Yerusholayim is breached.”⁵

footnotes:
⁵ Nechemyah 1:3.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:3:4)


**וְעֵת לִבְנוֹת.** שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, וּבְנִיתִיהָ כִּימֵי עוֹלָם:


**And a time to build.** As it is stated, “and I will build it up as in the days of old.”⁶

footnotes:
⁶ Amos 9:11.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:4:1)


**עֵת לִבְכּוֹת.** בְּתִשְׁעָה בְאָב:


**A time to weep.** On the ninth of [Menachem] Av.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:4:2)


**וְעֵת לִשְׂחוֹק.** לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "אָז יִמָּלֵא שְׂחוֹק פִּינוּ":


**And a time to laugh.** In the time to come, as it is stated, “Then our mouth will be filled with laughter.”⁷

footnotes:
⁷ Tehillim 126:2.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:4:3)


**עֵת סְפוֹד.** בִּימֵי אֵבֶל:


**A time to mourn.** In the days of mourning.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:4:4)


**וְעֵת רְקוֹד.** בַּחֲתָנִים וְכַלּוֹת:


**And a time to dance.** With bridegrooms and brides.⁸

footnotes:
⁸ The Gra explains the reason why this verse does not have parallel structure, i.e., why is does not state עת לספוד ועת לרקוד, i.e., with a lamed as in the beginning עת לבכות ועת לשחוק. He says that it hints to the halachah stated in Maseches Kesuvos 17a, that on two occasions one interrupts his Torah study [= למד], lamed, to attend a wedding [רקוד] or to attend a funeral [ספוד].



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:5:1)


**עֵת לְהַשְׁלִיךְ אֲבָנִים.** בַּחוּרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מֻשְׁלָכִים בְּחֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת, "תִּשְׁתַּפֵּכְנָה אַבְנֵי־קֹדֶשׁ:"


**A time to cast away stones.** The youths of Yisroel scattered during the destruction of the Beis [Hamikdosh], as it is stated, “sacred gems are scattered.”⁹

footnotes:
⁹ Eichah 4:1.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:5:2)


**וְעֵת כְּנוֹס** אוֹתָם מִן הַגּוֹלָה, "וְהוֹשִׁיעָם ה' אֱלֹהֵיהֶם בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא כְּצֹאן עַמּוֹ כִּי אַבְנֵי־נֵזֶר מִתְנוֹסְסוֹת עַל־אַדְמָתוֹ:


**And a time to gather them** from the exile, as it is stated, “And Adonoy their God will save His people like sheep on that day, for the stones of the crown will be exalted over His land.”¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ Zecharyah 9:16.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:5:3)


**עֵת לַחֲבוֹק.** "כִּי כַּאֲשֶׁר יִדְבַּק הָאֵזוֹר וְגוֹ'":


**A time to embrace.** [As it is stated,] “For, just as a belt is fastened, etc.”¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ Yirmiyahu 13:11.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:5:4)


**וְעֵת לִרְחֹק מְחַבֵּק.** "וְרִחַק ה' אֶת־הָאָדָם":


**And a time to refrain from embracing.** [As it is stated,] “And Adonoy will drive the people far away.”¹²

footnotes:
¹² Yeshayahu 6:12.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:6:1)


**עֵת לְבַקֵּשׁ.** כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, אֶת־הָאֹבֶדֶת אֲבַקֵּשׁ. לְעִנְיַן נִדְחֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**A time to seek.** As the matter is stated, “I will seek the lost,”¹³ concerning the scattered among Yisroel.

footnotes:
¹³ Yechezkeil 34:16.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:6:2)


**וְעֵת לְאַבֵּד.** וְעֵת שֶׁאִבְּדָם בַּגּוֹלָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "וַאֲבַדְתֶּם בַּגּוֹיִם".


**And a time to abandon.** And a time when He causes them to perish in the exile, as it is stated, “And you will become lost among the nations.”¹⁴

footnotes:
¹⁴ Vayikra 26:38.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:6:3)


**עֵת לִשְׁמוֹר.** "יְבָרֶכְךָ ה' וְיִשְׁמְרֶךָ", כְּשֶׁאַתֶּם עוֹשִׂים רְצוֹנוֹ:


**A time to preserve.** [As it is stated,] “May Adonoy bless you and safeguard you,”¹⁵ when you do His will.

footnotes:
¹⁵ Bamidbar 6:24.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:6:4)


**וְעֵת לְהַשְׁלִיךְ.** "וַיַּשְׁלִכֵם אֶל־אֶרֶץ אַחֶרֶת":


**And a time to discard.** [As it is stated,] “And He cast them to another land.”¹⁶

footnotes:
¹⁶ Devarim 29:27.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:7:1)


**עֵת לִקְרוֹעַ.** מַלְכוּת בֵּית דָּוִד, "וָאֶקְרַע אֶת־הַמַמְלָכָה וְגוֹ'":


**A time to rip apart.** The kingship of the House of Dovid,¹⁷ [as it is stated,] “And I tore the kingship, etc.”¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁷ When Bnei Yisroel act in an evil manner.
¹⁸ I Melochim 14:8.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:7:2)


**וְעֵת לִתְפוֹר.** "וְהָיוּ לַאֲחָדִים בְּיָדֶךָ. . . וְלֹא יֵחָצוּ עוֹד לִשְׁתֵּי מַמְלָכוֹת":


**And a time to sew.** [As it is stated,] “And they will become united in your hand;”¹⁹ [and] “neither shall they be divided any longer into two kingdoms.”²⁰

footnotes:
¹⁹ Yechezkeil 37:17.
²⁰ Ibid. Verse 22.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:7:3)


**עֵת לַחֲשׁוֹת.** פְּעָמִים שֶׁאָדָם שׁוֹתֵק וּמְקַבֵּל שָׂכָר, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "וַיִּדֹּם אַהֲרֹן", וְזָכָה שֶׁנִּתְיַחֵד הַדִּבּוּר עִמּוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "וַיְדַבֵּר ה' אֶל־אַהֲרֹן. . . יַיִן וְשֵׁכָר אַל־תֵּשְׁתְּ":


**A time to be silent.** These are the times when a person is silent and receives a reward,²¹ as it is stated, “and Aharon was silent,”²² and he [therefore] merited that the Divine speech addressed him personally, as it is stated, “And Adonoy spoke to Aharon, [saying] ‘Do not drink intoxicating wine.’”²³

footnotes:
²¹ E.g., when a person is insulted and yet remains silent. (Metsudas Dovid)
²² Vayikra 10:3.
²³ Ibid. Verse 8.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:7:4)


**וְעֵת לְדַבֵּר.** "אָז יָשִׁיר משֶׁה"; "וַתָּשַׁר דְּבוֹרָה"; "קְחוּ עִמָּכֶם דְּבָרִים":


**And a time to speak.** [As it is stated,] “Then Moshe sang,”²⁴ [and,] “And Devorah sang,”²⁵ [and,] “Take words with you.”²⁶

footnotes:
²⁴ Shemos 15:1.
²⁵ Shoftim 5:1.
²⁶ Hoshei’a 14:3.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:8:1)


**עֵת לֶאֱהֹב.** "וַאֲהֵבְךָ:"


**A time to love.** [As it is stated,] “And He will love you.”²⁷

footnotes:
²⁷ Devarim 7:13.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:8:2)


**וְעֵת לִשְׂנֹא.** שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "כָּל־רָעָתָם בַּגִּלְגָּל כִּי־שָׁם שְׂנֵאתִים":


**And a time to hate.** [As] it is stated, “All their evil is in Gilgal; for there I hated them.”²⁸

footnotes:
²⁸ Hoshei’a 9:15.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:9:1)


**מַה־יִּתְרוֹן הָעוֹשֶׂה.** מַה־יִּתְרוֹן שֶׁל עוֹשֶׂה רַע בְּכָל־שֶׁהוּא עָמֵל? גַּם הוּא עִתּוֹ יָבֹא וְהַכֹּל אָבַד:


**What benefit [then] has the worker.** What is the benefit of the one who does evil, in all that he labors? He too, his time will come, and all [his ill gotten gains] will be lost.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:10:1)


**הָעִנְיָן.** הַמִּנְהָג:


**The matter.** The behavior.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:10:2)


**לַעֲנוֹת.** לְהִתְנַהֵג:


**To be afflicted.** To behave.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:11:1)


**יָפֶה בְעִתּוֹ.** בְּעֵת הַטּוֹבָה, יָפֶה הוּא לָבֹא תַשְׁלוּם שְׂכַר מַעֲשֵׂה הַטּוֹב, וּבְעֵת הָרָעָה, רְאוּיָה הִיא לְתַשְׁלוּם מַעֲשֵׂה הָרָע:


**Beautiful in its time.** In its proper time, it is beautiful that reward be given for good deeds, but at the time of evil, it is appropriate for meting out punishment for evil deeds.²⁹

footnotes:
²⁹ Alternatively, “beautiful in its time,” refers to God’s provision of sustenance to all in a timely fashion (Sforno). Or, everything He made is “beautiful when used in its designated time.” (Metsudas Dovid)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:11:2)


גַּם אֶת־הָעֹלָם **נָתַן בְּלִבָּם וְגוֹ'.** גַּם אֶת־חָכְמַת הָעוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר נָתַן בְּלֵב הַבְּרִיּוֹת, לֹא נָתַן הַכֹּל בְּלֵב כָּל־אֶחָד וְאֶחָד, אֶלָּא זֶה קְצַת וְזֶה קְצַת, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִמְצָא הָאָדָם אֶת־כָּל־מַעֲשֵׂה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לָדַעַת אוֹתוֹ, וְלֹא יֵדַע אֶת־עֵת פְּקֻדָּתוֹ, וּבַמֶּה יִכָּשֵׁל, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתֵּן לֵב לָשׁוּב, שֶׁיִּדְאַג וְיֹאמַר: "הַיּוֹם אוֹ מָחָר אָמוּת". וּלְכַךְ, כָּתוּב כַּאן "הָעֹלָם" חָסֵר, לְשׁוֹן הָעֲלָמָה, שֶׁאִם יֵדַע הָאָדָם יוֹם מִיתָתוֹ קְרוֹבָה, לֹא יִבְנֶה בַיִת וְלֹא יִטַּע כֶּרֶם. לְכַךְ הוּא אוֹמֵר שֶׁהַכֹּל עָשָׂה יָפֶה בְעִתּוֹ; זֶה שֶׁיֵּשׁ עֵת לְמִיתָה, דָּבָר יָפֶה הוּא, שֶׁסּוֹמֵךְ הָאָדָם לוֹמַר: "שֶׁמָּא עֲדַיִן עֵת מִיתָתִי רָחוֹק", וּבוֹנֶה בַיִת וְנוֹטֵעַ כֶּרֶם, וְזוּ יָפָה, שֶׁנֶּעְלַם מִן הַבְּרִיּוֹת:


Even [a sense of] the external world **He has set in their heart, etc.** Also the wisdom of the world that He instilled into the hearts of mankind, He did not instill it all into each one’s heart, rather a little to this one and a little to that one, in order that man should not fully grasp the workings of the Holy One, Blessed Is He, to know it; [and thereby] he will not know the day of his visitation [death] and on what he will stumble, in order that he put his heart to repent, so that he will be concerned and say [to himself], “Today or tomorrow I will die.” Therefore, ‘הָעֹלָם’ is written here defectively [i.e., without a ‘*vav*’], an expression of “hidden,” for if man knew that the day of his death was near, he would neither build a house nor plant a vineyard.³⁰ Therefore, he says that, “He has made everything beautiful in its time.” The fact that there is a time for death is a beautiful thing, for a person optimistically says [to himself], “Perhaps my death is far off,” and he builds a house and plants a vineyard; and it is beautiful that it is concealed from people.³¹

footnotes:
³⁰ Alternatively, “העלם” means eternity and the verse is translated as “even a sense of eternity [העלם] He has set in their heart, etc.” and therefore they act as if they will live forever. (Ibn Ezra)
³¹ Alternatively, העלם is referring to forgetfulness.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:12:1)


**יָדַעְתִּי.** עַתָּה, הוֹאִיל וְנֶעְלַם עֵת הַפְּקֻדָּה, כִּי אֵין טוֹב בַּבְּרִיּוֹת כִּי אִם לִשְׂמוֹחַ בְּחֶלְקוֹ וְלַעֲשׂוֹת הַטּוֹב בְּעֵינֵי בוֹרְאוֹ בְּעוֹד שֶׁהוּא חָי:


**I know.** Now, since the time of visitation [death] is concealed, that there is nothing better for man than to rejoice with his portion and to do that which is good in his Creator’s eyes, while he is yet alive.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:13:1)


**וְרָאָה טוֹב.** תּוֹרָה וּמִצְוֹת:


**And enjoys the good.** The Torah and the commandments.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:14:1)


**יָדַעְתִּי כִּי כָּל־אֲשֶׁר יַעֲשֶׂה.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּמַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית, הוּא רָאוּי לִהְיוֹת לְעוֹלָם, וְאֵין לְשַׁנּוֹתוֹ, לֹא בְתוֹסֶפֶת וְלֹא בְגֵרָעוֹן, וּכְשֶׁנִּשְׁתַּנָּה, הָאֱלֹהִים צִוָּה וְעָשָׂה שֶׁיִּשְׁתַּנֶּה, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּירְאוּ מִלְּפָנָיו. אוֹקְיָנוֹס פָּרַץ גְּבוּלוֹ בְּדוֹר אֱנוֹשׁ וְהֵצִיף שְׁלִישׁוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם, וְהָאֱלֹהִים עָשָׂה שֶׁיִּרְאוּ מִלְּפָנָיו. שִׁבְעַת יָמִים נִשְׁתַּנָּה הִלּוּךְ הַחַמָּה בְּדוֹר הַמַּבּוּל, לִזְרוֹחַ בְּמַעֲרָב וְלִשְׁקוֹעַ בְּמִזְרָח, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּרְאוּ מִלְּפָנָיו. חַמָּה חָזְרָה לַאֲחוֹרֶיהָ עֶשֶׂר מַעֲלוֹת בִּימֵי חִזְקִיָּהוּ, וּבִימֵי אָחָז אָבִיו, נִתְקַצֵּר הַיּוֹם וְנִתְרַבָּה הַלַּיְלָה בְיוֹם מוֹתוֹ, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא נִסְפָּד. כָּל־זֶה כְּדֵי שֶׁיִרְאוּ מִלְּפָנָיו. לְפִיכָךְ, אֵין טוֹב לָאָדָם לַעֲסוֹק אֶלָּא בְמִצְוָתוֹ וְלִירָא מִלְּפָנָיו:


**I know that whatever [God] does.** The Holy One, Blessed Is He, in the Creation, is fit to be eternal, and it cannot be changed, either by adding or by subtracting, and when it is changed, [it is because] God commanded and caused it to be changed, in order that they should fear Him. The ocean broke through its boundary in the generation of Enosh and inundated a third of the world, and God did this so that they would fear Him. Seven days, the course of the sun was changed in the generation of the flood, to rise in the west and set in the east, in order that they fear Him. [Also,] the sun went back ten degrees in the days of Chizkiyahu; and in the days of Achaz his father, the day was shortened and the night was lengthened on the day of his death, so that he should not be eulogized. All this was so that they would fear Him.³² Therefore, there is nothing better for a man to occupy himself with than with [fulfilling] His commandments and to fear Him.

footnotes:
³² See the Gemara in Maseches Shabbos 31b which states that God created His world only that men should fear Him, i.e., that they should have awe and reverence for Him.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:15:1)


**מַה־שֶּׁהָיָה כְּבָר הוּא.** מַה־שֶׁהָיָה מִלְּפָנֵינוּ, כְּבָר הוּא עָשׂוּי וּרְאִינוּהוּ אוֹ שְׁמַעְנוּהוּ מֵאֲחֵרִים שֶׁרָאוּהוּ, וְיֵשׁ לָנוּ לְהָעִיד עָלָיו שֶׁרָאִינוּ שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְבַקֵּשׁ אֶת־הַנִּרְדָּפִים. יַעֲקֹב נִרְדָּף, עֵשָׂו רוֹדֵף. "וָאֹהַב אֶת־יַעֲקֹב וְאֶת־עֵשָׂו שָׂנֵאתִי". מִצְרַיִם רוֹדְפִים אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵל; מִצְרַיִם טֻבְּעוּ בַיָּם, וְיִשְׂרָאֵל הָלְכוּ בְיָד רָמָה:


**That which already was.** That which was before us, was already done, and we either saw it or heard it from others who saw it, and we can attest to it, for we saw that the Holy One, Blessed Is He, seeks the pursued. Yaakov was pursued, Eisav was a pursuer, [and Scripture states,] “And I loved Yaakov. But I hated Eisav.”³³ The Egyptians pursued Yisroel; the Egyptians drowned in the sea, and [Bnei] Yisroel were victorious.

footnotes:
³³ Malachi 1:1-2.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:15:2)


**וַאֲשֶׁר.** עָתִיד לִהְיוֹת עוֹד בָּאַחֲרוֹנָה, הוּא דֻגְמַת מַה שֶׁכְּבָר הָיָה. כַּאֲשֶׁר בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה, כֵּן בָּאַחֲרוֹנָה. אֵין הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְשַׁנֶּה מִדּוֹתָיו בָּעוֹלָם:


**And that which is.** Destined to be at the end is a model of what already was. As it was in the beginning, so will it be at the end. The Holy One, Blessed Is He, does not change His standards [by which He sustains] the world.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:15:3)


**וְהָאֱלֹהִים יְבַקֵּשׁ אֶת־נִרְדָּף.** לִפָּרַע מִן הָרוֹדֵף, לְפִיכָךְ, מַה־יִּתְרוֹנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹשֵׂה הָרָעָה בַּאֲשֶׁר הוּא עָמֵל? הֲרֵי סוֹפוֹ לְהִשְׁתַּלֵּם:


**And God seeks the pursued.** To punish the pursuer. Therefore, what benefit is derived by one who does evil in what he labors? Ultimately he will have to pay [for it].



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:16:1)


**מְקוֹם הַמִּשְׁפָּט וְגוֹ'.** רָאִיתִי בְרוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ מְקוֹם לִשְׁכַּת הַגָּזִית בִּיְרוּשָׁלַיִם שֶׁהָיְתָה "מְלֵאֲתִי מִשְׁפָּט". שָׁמָּה יִשְׁפְּטוּ רֶשַׁע, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "רָאשֶׁיהָ בְּשֹׁחַד יִשְׁפֹּטוּ", וְרָאִיתִי פֻרְעֲנוּתָם:


**In the place of justice, etc.** I saw with the Holy Spirit, the place of the Chamber of Hewn Stone in Yerusholayim, which was, “full of justice,”³⁴ there they judge wickedly, as it is stated, “Her leaders judge for bribes,”³⁵ and I saw their punishment.

footnotes:
³⁴ Yeshayahu 1:21.
³⁵ Michah 3:11.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:16:2)


**מְקוֹם הַצֶּדֶק.** שַׁעַר הַתָּוֶךְ, שֶׁהָיָה מְקוֹם חִתּוּךְ הֲלָכוֹת:


**And in the place of righteousness.** The middle gate, which was the place where laws were established.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:16:3)


**שָׁמָה הָרֶשַׁע.** שָׁם יֵשְׁבוּ שַׂרְסְכִים, רַב־סָרִיס, נֵרְגַל שַׂרְאֶצֶר, רַב־מָג, וּנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר וְחֵילוֹתָיו, וְדָנִין אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּיִסּוּרִים קָשִׁים וּמִשְׁפְּטֵי מָוֶת:
