**There was wickedness.** There sat Sarsachim, the chief captain, Nergal Saretzer, the chief officer, and Nevuchadnetzar and his armies,³⁶ and they judged Yisroel with harsh tortures and death sentences.

footnotes:
³⁶ See Yirmiyahu 39:3.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:16:4)


**שָׁמָּה הָרָשַׁע.** טַעֲמוֹ לְמַעֲלָה, לוֹמַר שֶׁהוּא שֵׁם דָּבָר כְּמוֹ הָרֶשַׁע (וְהָיָה רָאוּי לִנָּקֵד בְּסֶגּוֹ"ל הָרֵי"שׁ, אֶלָּא לְפִי שֶׁהוּא סוֹף פָּסוּק, נֶהְפַּךְ לִנָּקֵד קָמַ"ץ גָּדוֹל, וְאַף־עַל־פִּי שֶׁבְּכָל־מָקוֹם לֹא מָצִינוּ לוֹ עוֹד שֶׁמִּתְהַפֵּךְ בְּאֶתְנַחְתָּא וְסוֹף פָּסוּק):


**There was wickedness.** The accent mark is above [i.e., before the last syllable], indicating that it is a noun like [the preceding] ‘הָרֶשַׁע [wickedness].’³⁷ (It should have been vowelized with a *segol* under the *resh*, but since it is at the end of the verse, it is changed to be vowelized with a *kamatz gadol*, although we do not find another instance [of this word]³⁸, and the pei of the second פצע is vowelized with a kamatz because of the *esnachta*. (*Sifsei Chachomim*) where it changes in the case of an *esnachta* or a *sof pasuk*).

footnotes:
³⁷ Alternatively, הרשע is “the wicked person.” (Targum)
³⁸ But we do find instances of other words that change, as in Shemos 21:24, it states “פצע תחת פצע [=a wound for a wound],” the pei of the first “פצע” is vowelized with a segol



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:17:1)


**אָמַרְתִּי אֲנִי בְּלִבִּי וְגוֹ'.** לְפִיכָךְ אֲנִי אוֹמֵר, אֶת־הַכֹּל שׁוֹפֵט הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְאַחַר זְמַן; וְאַף־עַל־פִּי שֶׁמִּתְעַכֵּב הַדָּבָר, סוֹפוֹ לָבֹא אֶל שַׁעְתּוֹ. כִּי שָׁעָה יֵשׁ לְכָל־חֵפֶץ, אַף לְפֻרְעָנוּת. וְלִפְקֻדַּת הַדִּין יֵשׁ עֵת מָתַי יָבֹא:


**I said in my heart, etc.** Therefore, I say everything is judged by the Holy One, Blessed Is He, in due time, and even though the matter [appears] delayed, it will eventually reach its time, for there is a time for everything, even for retribution and for the visitation of judgement there is a time that comes.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:17:2)


**וְעַל כָּל־הַמַּעֲשֶׂה שֶׂעָשָׂה** הָאָדָם, **שָׁם** יִשְׁפְּטוּהוּ בְּבֹא עֵת הַפְּקֻדָּה. שָׁם בְּאוֹתוֹ הָעֵת נִתָּן זְמַן לְכָל־הַמַּעֲשֶׂה לְהִשָּׁפֵט עָלָיו. אַבַּב חוּטְרֵי מִילֵי וְאַבֵּי דָרֵי חוּשְׁבְּנָא:


**And for every deed that man did, they will judge him there when the time of the visitation arrives; there at that time, a time is given for every deed, to be judged for it.** At the gate of the fold there are words, but in the stall are there is strict accounting.³⁹

footnotes:
³⁹ See Maseches Shabbos 32a. The shepherd waits until the goats are by the gate of the fold, and then he rebukes and punishes them.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:18:1)


**אָמַרְתִּי אֲנִי בְּלִבִּי.** בִּרְאוֹתִי כָל־זֹאת:


**I said to myself.** When I observed all this.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:18:2)


**עַל־דִּבְרַת בְּנֵי הָאָדָם.** שֶׁאָחֲזוּ לָהֶם מִדַּת גַּאֲוָה לִנְהוֹג שְׂרָרָה וְרַבָּנוּת בִּקְטַנִּים מֵהֶם:


**Concerning the state of man.** Who adopted the trait of haughtiness, to exert rulership and superiority over those smaller than they.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:18:3)


**לְבָרָם.** (סְפָרִים אֲחֵרִים: לְבָרְרָם) הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְהוֹדִיעָם שֶׁאֵין שְׂרָרוֹתָם כְּלוּם, וּלְהַרְאוֹתָם שֶׁהֵם, וְאַף הַשָּׂרִים וְהַמְּלָכִים:


**[God] has set them apart.** (Other texts: **[God] should clarify for them.**) The Holy One, Blessed Is He, to notify them that their rulership is nought, and to show them and also the princes and the kings —


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:18:4)


**בְּהֵמָה הֵמָּה לָהֶם.** כִּשְׁאָר בְּהֵמָה וְחַיָּה הֵמָּה לְעַצְמָם:


**They themselves are as beast.** Like other beasts and animals they are to themselves.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:19:1)


**כִּי מִקְרֶה בְנֵי־הָאָדָם וְגוֹ'.** הוּא טַעַם הַדָּבָר, אֲשֶׁר נָתַן הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מִקְרֶה וּפֶגַע לִבְנֵי אָדָם, וְיֵשׁ מִקְרֶה וּפֶגַע לִבְהֵמָה, וּמִקְרֶה אֶחָד לִשְׁנֵיהֶם נָתַן, כִּי, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁזֶּה מֵת, כַּךְ זֶה מֵת:


**For the fate of men, etc.** This is the reason for the matter, that the Holy One, Blessed Is He, gave a fate and a mishap to mankind, and there is a fate and a mishap to the beasts, and He gave one fate to them both, for just as this one dies, so does that one die.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:19:2)


**וּמוֹתַר הָאָדָם מִן־הַבְּהֵמָה אָיִן.** וְיִתְרוֹנוֹ וְהַצְּלָחָתוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם יוֹתֵר מִן הַבְּהֵמָה אֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה מִשֶׁמֵּת, כִּי הַכֹּל נֶהְפָּךְ לִהְיוֹת הֶבֶל לָשׁוּב אֶל הֶעָפָר:


**And the superiority of man over beast is nil.** And the superiority and the success of man over beast is not apparent after he dies, for everything is converted to nothingness by returning to the dust.⁴⁰

footnotes:
⁴⁰ This is true when a person spends his life only in the accumulation of wealth. (Metsudas Dovid)



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:21:1)


**מִי יוֹדֵעַ.** כְּמוֹ, "מִי יוֹדֵעַ יָשׁוּב". מִי הוּא אֲשֶׁר מֵבִין וְנוֹתֵן לֵב שֶׁרוּחַ בְּנֵי אָדָם הִיא הָעוֹלָה לְמַעְלָה וְעוֹמֶדֶת בְּדִין, וְרוּחַ הַבְּהֵמָה הִיא הַיּוֹרֶדֶת לְמַטָּה לָאָרֶץ, וְאֵין לָהּ לִתֵּן דִּין וְחֶשְׁבּוֹן, וְצָרִיךְ שֶׁלֹּא לְהִתְנַהֵג כִּבְהֵמָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ מַקְפֶּדֶת עַל מַעֲשֶׂיהָ:


**Who knows.** As in, “Whoever knows, let him repent,”⁴¹ [i.e., he] who understands, knows that “the spirit of man” ascends on high and stands in judgment, “and the spirit of the beast” descends to the earth, and does not have to give a justification and reckoning.⁴² [Therefore,] one must not behave like a beast which does not [have to] care about its deeds.

footnotes:
⁴¹ Yoel 2:14.
⁴² However, there are a very few who understand and know this distinction which differentiates the spirit of man from beast. (Metsudas Dovid)



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:22:1)


**וְרָאִיתִי.** בְכָל־אֵלֶּה:


**I perceived.** In all of these.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:22:2)


**כִּי אֵין טוֹב.** לָאָדָם:


**That there is nothing better.** For man.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:22:3)


**מֵאֲשֶׁר יִשְׂמַח הָאָדָם בְּמַעֲשָׂיו.** מִיְגִיעַ כַּפָּיו יִשְׂמַח וְיֹאכַל, וְלֹא לְהַרְחִיב כִּשְׁאוֹל נַפְשׁוֹ לַחֲמוֹד לְהִתְעַשֵּׁר, לְהַרְבּוֹת לֹא לוֹ:


**That man should rejoice in his work.** He should rejoice and eat in the toil of his hands, but not to widen his desire like the grave, to covet riches, to accumulate that which is not rightfully his.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:22:4)


**כִּי הוּא חֶלְקוֹ.** יְגִיעַ כַּפָּיו הוּא הַחֵלֶק הַנִּתָּן לוֹ מִשָּׁמַיִם, וּבוֹ יִשְׂמַח:


**For that is his lot.** The toil of his hands, that is the portion given him from Heaven, and with it he should rejoice.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 3:22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/3:22:5)


**כִּי מִי יְבִיאֶנּוּ לִרְאוֹת.** לְאַחַר שֶׁמֵּת, בְּמֶה שֶׁיִּהְיֶה לְבָנָיו, אִם יַצְלִיחוּ גַם הֵם בָּעשֶׁר שֶׁאָסַף הוּא וְהֵנִיחַ לָהֶם, אוֹ לֹא יַצְלִיחוּ:


**For who will bring him to see.** After he dies, what his children will have; if they, too, will be successful with the wealth that he accumulated and left over for them, or whether they will not prosper.⁴³

footnotes:
⁴³ Alternatively, for who will bring him to see the reward and punishment after death.



## Chapter 4


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:1:1)


**וְשַׁבְתִּי אֲנִי וָאֶרְאֶה.** בְּרוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ:


**And I returned and considered.** By means of the Holy Spirit.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:1:2)


**אֶת־כָּל־הָעֲשֻׁקִים.** הַנַּעֲשִׂים עֲשׁוּקִים בְּגֵיהִנֹּם בַּמַּעֲשִׂים אֲשֶׁר נַעֲשִׂים:


**All the oppressions.** Who were made to be oppressed in Gehinnom for the deeds that were committed.¹

footnotes:
¹ Alternatively, one cannot rejoice in view of the injustice and robbery committed in the world. (Ibn Ezra)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:1:3)


**תַּחַת הַשָּׁמֶשׁ.** תַּחַת חֲלִיפִים שֶׁל הַתּוֹרָה:


**Under the sun.** תַּחַת [meaning] in exchange for the Torah.²

footnotes:
² See above 2:18.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:1:4)


**וְהִנֵּה דִּמְעַת הָעֲשֻׁקִים.** בּוֹכִים עַל נַפְשׁוֹתֵיהֶם הָעֲשׁוּקוֹת בְּיַד מַלְאֲכֵי מַשְׁחִית וְאַכְזָרִים, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר, "עֹבְרֵי בְּעֵמֶק הַבָּכָא מַעְיָן יְשִׁיתוּהוּ"; אֵלּוּ יוֹרְדֵי גֵיהִנֹּם. וְאַף מִקְרָא זֶה כַּךְ נִדְרָשׁ בְּסִפְרֵי:


**And behold, tears of the oppressed.** Weeping for their souls, which are oppressed in the hand of the destructive and cruel angels, and similarly it says, “Transgressors in the valley of weeping, transform it into a fountain,”³ these are the ones who descend to Gehinnom. And this verse too is expounded in this manner in Sifre.

footnotes:
³ Tehillim 84:7.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:1:5)


**וּמִיַּד עשְׁקֵיהֶם כֹּחַ.** עוֹשְׁקֵיהֶם מַכְרִיחִים וְתוֹקְפִים אוֹתָם בְּכֹחַ:


**And from the hand of their oppressors [flows the] power.** Their oppressors overwhelm them and attack them with force.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:2:1)


**שֶׁכְּבָר מֵתוּ.** עַד שֶׁלֹּא שָׁלַט בָּהֶם יֵצֶר הָרַע הַזֶּה לִדְחוֹתָם מִן הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, כְּגוֹן אָבוֹת הָרִאשׁוֹנִים, שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲנָה משֶׁה אֶלָּא עַל יְדֵיהֶן, וּכְגוֹן דָּוִד אָבִי, שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲנֵיתִי אֲנִי בְכ"ד רְנָנוֹת עַד שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי, "זָכְרָה לְחַסְדֵי דָּוִד עַבְדֶּךָ":


**Who already died.** Before the evil inclination overpowered them to push them back from the Holy One, Blessed Is He,⁴ like the early Patriarchs,⁵) for Moshe was answered only through them [i.e., their merit].⁶) And like my father Dovid, for I was not answered with twenty-four praises⁷) until I said, “Remember the kind deeds of Your servant Dovid.”⁸

footnotes:
⁴ Alternatively, I am able to determine the praise of those who died [ושבח אני את המתים], by observing the conduct and type of life style of their children who are alive [מן החיים]. (Yalkot Eliezer)
⁵ Rashi answers the apparent contradiction between this verse which praises the dead and below [9:4]. Koheles states “for a live dog is better than a dead lion.” (Sifsei Chachomim
⁶ See Maseches Shabbos 30a. Moshe said many prayers and God did not answer, but when he mentioned the merit of the Patriarchs in his prayers, he was immediately answered. (Sifsei Chachomim
⁷ See Maseches Shabbos 30a. The Gemara there relates that the gates in the Bais Hamikdosh clung to each other when Shlomo attempted to place the Holy Ark into the Kodesh Hakadoshim. He said twenty-four expressions of entreaty and was not answered until he mentioned the kind deeds of Dovid. (Sifsei Chachomim
⁸ II Divrei Hayomim 6:42.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:3:1)


**עֲדֶן.** עֲדַיִן:


**Yet.** Yet עֲדַיִן=עֲדֶן.⁹

footnotes:
⁹ ‘עדן’ is a shortened version of עד הנה [=as yet]. (Ibn Ezra)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:3:2)


**אֲשֶׁר לֹא רָאָה אֶת־הַמַּעֲשֶׂה.** רָאִיתִי בְמִדְרַשׁ קֹהֶלֶת, אֵלּוּ תתקע"ד דּוֹרוֹת שֶׁקֻּמְּטוּ לְהִבָּרְאוֹת וְלֹא נִבְרְאוּ:


**Who has not seen the [evil] deed.** I saw in Midrash Koheles that these are the 974 generations that were decreed to be created, but were not created.¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ Of the one thousand generations that were decreed to be created before the giving of the Torah, 974 were not created. However, 26 generations after God created the world He gave the Torah. There were ten generations from Adam to No’ach, ten generations from No’ach to Avrohom, and six generations from Avrohom to Moshe. Thus 974 + 26 equals one thousand generations. See Rashi in Maseches Chagigah 14a.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:4:1)


**וְרָאִיתִי אֲנִי אֶת־כָּל־עָמָל.** הֵן הָעֲבֵרוֹת, שֶׁהֵן עָמָל בְּעֵינֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**And I observed that all labor.** These are the sins, which are toil in the eyes of the Holy One, Blessed Is He.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:4:2)


**וְאֵת כָּל־כִּשְׁרוֹן הַמַּעֲשֶׂה.** שֶׁאֵינוּ לְשֵׁם שָׁמַיִם, אֶלָּא לְקִנְאַת אִישׁ מֵרֵעֵהוּ, שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם הָבֶל:


**And all skillful work.** Which is not for the sake of Heaven, but for one’s envy of his friend, both of which are vanity.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:4:3)


**כִּי הִיא קִנְאַת אִישׁ.** אֲשֶׁר הִיא קִנְאַת אִישׁ מֵרֵעֵהוּ:


**Is the result of man’s envy.** Which is the result of man’s envy of his neighbor.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:5:1)


**הַכְּסִיל.** הָרָשָׁע:


**The fool.** The wicked person.¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ Alternatively, “the fool.” (Targum)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:5:2)


**חוֹבֵק אֶת־יָדָיו.** וְאֵינוֹ יָגֵעַ, וְאֵינוֹ אוֹכֵל אֶלָּא מִן הַגָּזֶל:


**Folds his hands.** And does not toil, and he eats only from robbery.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:5:3)


**וְאוֹכֵל אֶת־בְּשָׂרוֹ.** לְיוֹם הַדִּין שֶׁרוֹאֶה צַדִּיקִים בְּכָבוֹד, וְהוּא נִדּוֹן. כַּךְ נִדְרָשׁ בְּסִפְרֵי:


**And eats his own flesh.** On the Day of Judgement, when he sees the righteous bestowed with honor, while he is being judged.¹² It is expounded in this manner in Sifre.

footnotes:
¹² Alternatively, because he did not toil, he eventually dies from starvation (Ibn Ezra); or בשרו refers to “his relatives” that suffer on his account. See Rashi below 5:5 and also in Yeshayahu 58:7.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:6:1)


**טוֹב מְלֹא כַף נָחַת.** לִהְיוֹת קוֹנֶה נְכָסִים מְעַט מִיגִיעוֹ, שֶׁיְּהֵא בָהֶן נַחַת רוּחַ לְיוֹצְרוֹ:


**Better is a handful [earned] in contentment.** To acquire few possessions, but with his toil, so that his Creator should have satisfaction from them.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:6:2)


**מִמְּלֹא חָפְנַיִם.** נְכָסִים הַרְבֵּה בַּעֲבֵרָה, שֶׁהוּא עָמָל וְעַצְבוּת רוּחַ לִפְנֵי הַמָּקוֹם:


**Than two handfuls [earned].** Many possessions [acquired] through sin,¹³ which is toil and grief to the Omnipresent.

footnotes:
¹³ Alternatively, the verse refers to giving charity, i.e., better a lesser amount given to a poor person but with a pleasant demeanor, than giving twice as much, but causing the poor man to feel uncomfortable. (Nachal Eshkol)



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:7:1)


**תַּחַת הַשָּׁמֶשׁ.** כְּמוֹ "תַּחַת הַשָּׁמָיִם":


**Under the sun.** Is the same as, “under the heavens.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:8:1)


**יֵשׁ אֶחָד וְאֵין שֵׁנִי.** יֵשׁ לְךָ אָדָם שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה דְבָרָיו בִּיחִידִי:


**There is one who is alone with none other beside him.** There is a person who does his work alone.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:8:2)


**גַּם בֵּן וָאָח אֵין לוֹ.** אִם תַּלְמִיד חָכָם הוּא, אֵינוֹ קוֹנֶה לוֹ תַלְמִיד שֶׁהוּא כְבֵן, וְלֹא חָבֵר שֶׁהוּא כְּאָח. וְאִם רַוָּק הוּא, אֵינוּ נוֹשֵׂא אִשָּׁה לִהְיוֹת לוֹ כְאָח לְעֵזֶר וּלְהוֹלִיד בֵּן. וְאִם סוֹחֵר הוּא, אֵינוֹ קוֹנֶה לוֹ שֻׁתָּפִים, וְיוֹצֵא לְדֶרֶךְ יְחִידִי:


**He has neither son nor brother.** If he is Torah scholar, he neither acquires for himself a disciple, who is like a son, nor a companion, who is like a brother. And if he is a bachelor, he does not take a wife, to be to him like a brother, as a helper and to beget a son. And if he is a merchant, he does not acquire partners for himself, but he goes out on the road by himself.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:8:3)


**וְאֵין קֵץ לְכָל־עֲמָלוֹ.** יָגֵעַ בְּגִירְסָא, וְאִם סוֹחֵר הוּא, עָמֵל בִּפְרַקְמַטְיָא:


**Yet there is no end to his labor.** He toils in study, and if he is a merchant, he labors in business.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:8:4)


**גַּם־עֵינוֹ לֹא תִשְׂבַּע עשֶׁר.** לֹא יְהֵא שָׂבֵעַ בְּטַעֲמֵי תוֹרָה, שֶׁהַרְבֵּה תוֹרָה לוֹמֵד אָדָם מִתַּלְמִידָיו. וּלְעִנְיַן הַמָּמוֹן, רוֹדֵף תָּמִיד אַחַר הַמָּמוֹן:


**Neither is his eye ever satisfied with riches.** He will not be sated from the insights of Torah, for a person learns much Torah from his students, and regarding money, he constantly pursues money.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:8:5)


**וּלְמִי אֲנִי עָמֵל.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁאֵינִי מַעֲמִיד תַּלְמִידִים, וְאֵינִי נוֹשֵׂא אִשָּׁה לְהוֹלִיד בָּנִים:


**And for whom do I labor.** Since I do not develop students, and I do not take a wife to beget children.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:9:1)


**טוֹבִים הַשְּׁנַיִם.** לְכָל־דָּבָר:


**Two are better.** In all aspects.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:9:2)


**מִן־הָאֶחָד.** לְפִיכָךְ, יִקְנֶה לוֹ אָדָם חָבֵר וְיִשָּׂא אִשָּׁה, אֲשֶׁר יֵשׁ לָהֶם יוֹתֵר רֶוַח בַּעֲמָלָם. הַרְבֵּה מְלָאכָה נַעֲשֵׂית בִּשְׁנַיִם, שֶׁאֵין הַיָּחִיד מַתְחִיל בָּהּ לְבַדּוֹ:


**Than one.** Therefore, a person should acquire for himself a companion and marry a woman, for they have a greater return in their labor. Much work is accomplished by two, because [there are tasks that] an individual would not start on his own.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:10:1)


**כִּי אִם־יִפֹּלוּ.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ. וּלְעִנְיַן הַמִּשְׁנָה, אִם תָּקְפָה עָלָיו מִשְׁנָה שֶׁלּוֹ, חֲבֵרוֹ מַחֲזִירָהּ לוֹ, אוֹ אִם יִכָּשֵׁל וְלֹא דִקְדֵּק אֶת־אֲשֶׁר שָׁמַע מִפִּי רַבּוֹ, בָּא חֲבֵירוֹ וּמַעֲמִידוֹ עַל הָאֱמֶת:


**For if they fall.** [To be understood] according to its apparent meaning. And regarding Torah studies, if his studies are too difficult for him, his companion will review them with him, or if he errs and is not exact in what he heard from his teacher, his companion will set him on the truth[ful course].¹⁴

footnotes:
¹⁴ The Gemara in Maseches Berachos 63b states that Torah scholars who sit alone and engage in Torah study become foolish and will eventually sin [by erring].


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:10:2)


**וְאִי לוֹ.** וְאוֹי לוֹ:


**But woe to him.** But woe to him וְאוֹי לוֹ=וְאִי לוֹ.¹⁵)

footnotes:
¹⁵ Rashi renders this interpretation because ואילו is spelled ‘full’, i.e. with a yud. (Sifsei Chachomim



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:11:1)


**וְחַם לָהֶם.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ, וּלְעִנְיַן זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה, מִתְחַמְּמִים זֶה מִזֶּה וּמוֹלִידִים:


**They can be warm.** [To be understood] according to its apparent meaning, regarding male and female, who are aroused by one another and procreate.¹⁶

footnotes:
¹⁶ Alternatively, these verses refers to the performance of a mitzvah, i.e., if each one performs a mitzvah on his own then if he slackens off, there is no one to encourage him and “uplift him.” However, when two people perform a mitzvah together, it adds “warmth” to their performance of the mitzvah. (Divrei Avrohom)



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:12:1)


**וְאִם יִתְקְפוֹ הָאֶחָד.** אִם בָּאוּ לִסְטִים עָלָיו לְתָקְפּוֹ אִם שְׁנַיִם הֵם, יַעַמְדוּ לְנֶגְדוֹ, וְכָל־שֶׁכֵּן אִם ג' הֵם, וְהַחוּט הַמְשֻׁלָּשׁ לֹא בִמְהֵרָה יִנָּתֵק. דָּבָר אַחֵר: מִי שֶׁהוּא תַּלְמִיד חָכָם, וּבְנוֹ וּבֶן־בְּנוֹ, שׁוּב אֵין תּוֹרָה פוֹסֶקֶת מִזַּרְעוֹ, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר, "לֹא יָמוּשׁוּ מִפִּיךָ וּמִפִּי זַרְעֲךָ וּמִפִּי זֶרַע זַרְעֲךָ". דָּבָר אַחֵר: חוּט הַמְשֻׁלָּשׁ בְּמִקְרָא וּבְמִשְׁנָה וּבְדֶרֶךְ אֶרֶץ לֹא בִמְהֵרָה הוּא חוֹטֵא. בְּפָנִים אֲחֵרִים נִדְרָשׁ בַּמִּדְרָשׁ: "יֵשׁ אֶחָד וְאֵין שֵׁנִי" לוֹ, אֲבָל אֵין סֵדֶר כָּל־הַמִּקְרָאוֹת הַלָּלוּ מִתְיַשֵּׁב עֲלֵיהֶם:


**And if an individual attacks.** If thieves came to attack him, if they are two, they will stand up to them, and certainly, if they are three, “and the threefold cord is not quickly severed.” Another explanation: Whoever is a Torah scholar, as well as his son and his grandson, the Torah will never cease from his seed, as [Scripture] states, “[My words] will not depart from your mouth nor from the mouth of your offspring nor from the mouth of your offspring’s offspring.”¹⁷ Another explanation: [And] “the threefold cord,” [i.e., one well versed] in Scripture, Mishnah, and [having] good manners, will not quickly sin. It is expounded in the Midrash in other ways, “There is one who is alone with none other beside”¹⁸ Him, but the sequence of all these verses does not correspond with their interpretations.

footnotes:
¹⁷ Yeshayahu 59:21.
¹⁸ Above Verse 8.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:13:1)


**טוֹב יֶלֶד מִסְכֵּן וְחָכָם.** זֶה יֵצֶר טוֹב, וְלָמָּה נִקְרָא שְׁמוֹ יֶלֶד? שֶׁאֵינוֹ בָא בְאָדָם עַד י"ג שָׁנָה:


**Better a poor but wise child.** This is the good inclination, and why is it called “child”? Because it comes to a person only at the age of thirteen years.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ Alternatively, this verse refers to Avrohom who had a Divine prophecy at the age of three years old and refused to bow before Nimrod’s idols. (Targum)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:13:2)


**מִסְכֵּן.** שֶׁאֵין הָאֵבָרִים שׁוֹמְעִים לוֹ כְּמוֹ לְיֵצֶר הָרָע:


**Poor.** Because the limbs do not obey it, as [they obey] the evil inclination.²⁰

footnotes:
²⁰ According to Rashi that this verse is referring to the good inclination and evil inclination, the expression “better” is somewhat difficult, because “better” implies that both are “good” and one is “better” that the other, but what “good” can be attributed to the evil inclination. The Amtachas Binyomin therefore suggests that the first mem of ממלך does not mean “than” rather it means “as a result of”, i.e., it is better to follow the good inclination “as a result of defeating, after a struggle, the evil inclination, as opposed to one who has not been exposed to the evil inclination, where the probability is a greater that he will be enticed.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:13:3)


**חָכָם.** שֶׁמַּשְׂכִּיל אֶת־הָאָדָם לְדֶרֶךְ טוֹבָה:


**Wise.** Which gives a person intelligence to [follow] the right way.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:13:4)


**מִמֶּלֶךְ זָקֵן וּכְסִיל.** זֶה יֵצֶר הָרַע, שֶׁהוּא שַׁלִּיט עַל כָּל־הָאֵבָרִים. **זָקֵן.** שֶׁמִּשָּׁעָה שֶׁנּוֹלָד הַוְּלָד, הוּא נָתוּן בּוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "לַפֶּתַח חַטָּאת רֹבֵץ": **וּכְסִיל.** שֶׁמַּתְעֵהוּ בְּדֶרֶךְ רָעָה, כַּךְ נִדְרָשׁ בַּמִּדְרָשׁ:


**Than an old and foolish king.** This is the evil inclination, which rules over all the limbs. “Old,” for when a child is born, it comes to him,²¹ as it is stated, “sin rests at the opening.”²² **And foolish.** Because it misleads him in the way of evil. It is expounded in the Midrash in this manner.

footnotes:
²¹ The evil inclination is referred to as “king” because all obey it, and “old” because it becomes part of man long before the good inclination.
²² Bereishis 4:7.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:13:5)


**אֲשֶׁר לֹא־יָדַע לְהִזָּהֵר עוֹד.** שֶׁהֲרֵי הִזְקִין, וְאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל תּוֹכֵחָה:


**Who no longer knows how to be vigilant.** Because he has aged and no longer accepts reproof.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:14:1)


**כִּי־מִבֵּית הָסוּרִים יָצָא לִמְלֹךְ.** מִמְּקוֹם הַטִּנֹּפֶת וְסִרְחוֹן, כְּדִמְתַרְגְּמִינָן "וַיִּבְאַשׁ" וּסְרִי:


**Even though he [the child] emerged from prison to be king.** From the place of filth and stench [=הָסוּרִים, as Targum renders, ‘וַיִּבְאַשׁ’, “and it became putrid וּסְרִי.”


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:14:2)


**כִּי גַּם בְּמַלְכוּתוֹ.** מִּשֶׁמָּלַךְ בְּאָדָם, נוֹלַד הָרָשׁ הַמִּסְכֵּן הַטּוֹב מִמֶּנּוּ, וּבָא מִתּוֹךְ טָהֳרָה וְלֹא מִתּוֹךְ טִנֹּפֶת הָרֶחֶם, כַּךְ דְּרָשׁוּהוּ בַמִּדְרָשׁ. דָבָר אַחֵר: "כִּי טוֹב יֶלֶד מִסְכֵּן וְגוֹ'" כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ. "אֲשֶׁר לֹא־יָדַע לְהִזָּהֵר עוֹד", שֶׁכְּבָר הִזְקִין בְּרִשְׁעוֹ וּכְסִילוּתו‍ֹ. "כִּי מִבֵּית הָאֲסוּרִים יָצָא לִמְלֹךְ", כִּי הַיֶּלֶד הַמִּסְכֵּן, סוֹפוֹ שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ עָלָיו שֶׁיָּצָא לִמְלֹךְ מִתּוֹךְ עָנְיוֹ וּמִבֵּית אֲסוּרָיו, שֶׁהֲרֵי סַמֶּ"ךְ שֶׁל הָסוּרִים נָקוּד רָפֵי, וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְּמוֹ "הָאֲסוּרִים". (סְפָרִים אֲחֵרִים: כְּמוֹ, "וְלֹא־יַהֵל שָׁם עֲרָבִי", כְּמוֹ יַאֲהֵל) שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ בְיוֹסֵף, שֶׁמָּלַךְ מִתּוֹךְ יְצִיאַת בֵּית הָאֲסוּרִים. וְכֵן דָּוִד, "אֲנִי לְקַחְתִּיךָ מִן־הַנָּוֶה מֵאַחַר הַצֹּאן":


**Even though in his own kingdom.** Since he reigned over man, the poor one was born; who was better than he, and who came out of purity, not out of the filth of the womb. So it was expounded in the Midrash. Another explanation: “Better a poor but wise child, etc.” [to be understood] according to its apparent meaning, “[one] who no longer knows to be vigilant,” for he has already aged in his wickedness and foolishness. “Even though he [the child] emerged from prison to be king.” For the poor child; it will ultimately be said about him that he went out to reign from the midst of his affliction and from his prison cell, for the *samech* of הָסוּרִים is punctuated without a *dagesh*, which is the equivalent of הָאֲסוּרִים. (Some editions: As in, “And no Arab will pitch יַהֵל his tent there,”²³ which is the same as “יַאֲהֵל.”) For so we find that Yoseif reigned following his being freed from prison, and so Dovid, “I have taken you from the sheepcote, from following the flocks.”²⁴

footnotes:
²³ Yeshayahu 13:20.
²⁴ II Shmuel 7:8.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:14:3)


**כִּי גַּם בְּמַלְכוּתוֹ נוֹלַד רָשׁ.** כִּי כָשֵׁר וְהָגוּן הוּא שֶׁיִּמְלֹךְ, כִּי גַּם בְּמַלְכוּתוֹ הוּא נֶהְפַּךְ מִמִּנְהַג הַשְּׂרָרָה וּמַקְטִין עַצְמוֹ אֵצֶל הַחֲכָמִים כְּמִדַּת הָרָשִׁים, וְכֵן, "וְעַיִר פֶּרֶא אָדָם יִוָּלֵד", שֶׁיֵּהָפֵךְ וְיִשְׁתַּנֶּה לוֹ מִמַּה שֶׁהָיָה כְעַיִר פֶּרֶא, וְיֵעָשֶׂה אָדָם:


**Even though in his own kingdom he was born a pauper.** For it is fitting and proper that he should reign, because even in his kingship, he changed from the tradition of the ruling class and he humbled himself to the sages, which is characteristic of the poor. And so, “and from [one who is like] a wild donkey, a man will be born,”²⁵ [meaning,] that he will be changed and converted from what he was, resembling a wild donkey, and he will become a man.

footnotes:
²⁵ Iyov 11:12.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:14:4)


**נוֹלַד.** נַעֲשָׂה, וּלְשׁוֹן הֹוֶה הוּא:


**He was born.** נוֹלַד means] “becomes,” and it is in the present tense.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:15:1)


**רָאִיתִי אֶת־כָּל־הַחַיִּים וְגוֹ'.** מָצָאתִי בְמִדְרַשׁ הַסֵּפֶר הַזֶּה: "זֶה דוֹר הַמַּבּוּל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בָּהֶם 'וּמִכָּל־הָחַי'":


**I observed that all the living, etc.** I found in the Midrash of this Book, that this refers to the generation of the flood, about which it is stated, “And of every living thing.”²⁶

footnotes:
²⁶ Bereishis 6:19.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:15:2)


**עִם הַיֶּלֶד הַשֵּׁנִי.** אֲשֶׁר יִתְקַיֵּם תַּחַת אוֹתוֹ הַדּוֹר, שֶׁהֵם נֹחַ וּבָנָיו:


**Were supportive of the young successor.** Who will exist instead of that generation, i.e., No’ach and his sons.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:16:1)


**אֵין־קֵץ לְכָל־הָעָם.** פָּרִים וְרָבִים הָיוּ יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "זַרְעָם נָכוֹן לִפְנֵיהֶם". אִשָּׁה מִתְעַבֶּרֶת וְיוֹלֶדֶת לִשְׁלשָׁה יָמִים. "יְשַׁלְּחוּ כַצֹּאן עֲוִילֵיהֶם":


**There was no limit to all the peoples.** They were fruitful and multiplied too much, as it is stated, “their children are [well established] in their sight,”²⁷ a woman would conceive and bear in three days, “They send out their infants [i.e., carefree] like sheep.”²⁸

footnotes:
²⁷ Iyov 21:8.
²⁸ Ibid. Verse 11.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:16:2)


**לְכֹל אֲשֶׁר־הָיָה לִפְנֵיהֶם.** אֵין קֵץ לְכָל־טוֹב שֶׁהָיָה לִפְנֵיהֶם, וְהַכֹּל אָבַד, וּבְרֶגַע שְׁאוֹל יֵחַתּוּ:


**To all of them whom he lead.** There was no end to all the good that they had, but all was lost, and in an instant descended to the grave.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:16:3)


**גַּם הָאַחֲרוֹנִים.** דּוֹר הַפְלָגָה:


**Even though [it is so now] those who come after [the next generation].** The generation of dispersion.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:16:4)


**לֹא יִשְׂמְחוּ.** גַּם הֵם בַּטּוֹב הַנִּתָּן בְּיָדָם:


**Will not rejoice.** Also they, with the good that was given into their hands.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:16:5)


**כִּי־גַּם־זֶה.** סוֹפוֹ שֶׁל הֶבֶל וְרַעְיוֹן רוּחַ כַּאֲשֶׁר אָדָם מְהַלֵּךְ אַחַר יִצְרוֹ:


**For this too.** Its end is that of vanity and frustration, when a person follows his [evil] inclination.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:17:1)


**שְׁמֹר רַגְלְךָ כַּאֲשֶׁר תֵּלֵךְ אֶל־בֵּית הָאֱלֹהִים.** הֵיאַךְ תֵּלֵךְ. אִם תָּבִיא תוֹדָה וְנִדְבַת שְׁלָמִים, הוּא הַטּוֹב, וּשְׁמֹר עַצְמְךָ, שֶׁלֹּא תִצְטָרֵךְ לֵילֵךְ בַּהֲבָאַת חַטָּאוֹת וַאֲשָׁמוֹת:


**Guard your foot when you go to the House of God.** [“Watch”] how you go, if you bring a thanksgiving offering or a peace offering, that is good.²⁹ But “watch” yourself that you should not have to go and bring sin offerings or guilt offerings.

footnotes:
²⁹ Alternatively, this verse refers to one’s behavior when entering the House of God, i.e., he should enter in a manner that reflects reverence for God; or, one should make sure that his shoes are clean before entering.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:17:2)


**וְקָרוֹב.** הֱוֵי לִשְׁמֹעַ דִּבְרֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, וֶהֱוֵי טוֹב וְקָרוֹב לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**Come near.** To obey the words of the Holy One , Blessed Is He, and be good and near the Holy One, Blessed Is He.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:17:3)


**מִתֵּת הַכְּסִילִים זָבַח.** שֶׁיֶּחֱטָא וְיָבִיא קָרְבָּן:


**Rather than offer sacrifices like the fools.** That he should sin and bring a sacrifice.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 4:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/4:17:4)


**כִּי־אֵינָם יוֹדְעִים לַעֲשׂוֹת רָע.** אֵין הַכְּסִיל מֵבִין שֶׁהוּא עוֹשֶׂה רַע לְעַצְמוֹ:


**For they do not know that they do evil.** The fool does not realize that he is harming himself.³⁰

footnotes:
³⁰ The fool thinks that it is okay to sin as long as he brings a sin offering. (Metsudas Dovid)



## Chapter 5


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:1:1)


**לְהוֹצִיא דָבָר לִפְנֵי הָאֱלֹהִים.** לְדַבֵּר קָשֶׁה כְּלַפֵּי מָעְלָה:


**To utter a word before God.** To speak critically toward [God] above.¹

footnotes:
¹ Alternatively, “do not let your heart be hasty” to voice complaints before God, “for He is in heaven and you are on earth,” i.e., He alone knows what is good for you and whatever he does is for the best. See Maseches Berachos 61a and Maharsha there.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:1:2)


**כִּי הָאֱלֹהִים בָּשָּׁמַיִם וְאַתָּה עַל־הָאָרֶץ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ חַלָּשׁ מִלְּמַעְלָה וְגִבּוֹר מִלְּמַטָּה, אֵימַת חַלָּשׁ עַל הַגִּבּוֹר, וְכָל־שֶׁכֵּן גִּבּוֹר לְמַעְלָה וְחַלָּשׁ לְמַטָּה:


**For God is in heaven and you are on earth.** And even if the weak one is above, and the strong one below, the fear of the weak one is upon the strong one, how much more so when it is the strong one [=God] Who is above and the weak one who is below.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:2:1)


**כִּי בָּא הַחֲלוֹם בְּרֹב עִנְיָן.** כִּי דֶרֶךְ הַחֲלוֹם לָבוֹא בְּרֹב הִרְהוּרִים שֶׁאָדָם מְעַיֵּן וּמְהַרְהֵר בַּיּוֹם, וְדֶרֶךְ קוֹל כְּסִיל לָבוֹא בְרֹב דְּבָרִים, כִּי בְּהַרְבּוֹתוֹ דְבָרִים, מוֹצִיא קוֹל שֶׁל כְּסִילוּת מִפִּיו, כִּי לֹא יֶחְדַּל פֶּשַׁע, לְכַךְ אֲנִי אוֹמֵר: "יִהְיוּ דְבָרֶיךָ מְעַטִּים":


**For a dream comes with many subjects.** For it is usual for a dream to come because of the many thoughts upon which a person ponders and thinks during the day, and it is usual for the speech of a fool to come with excessive words, because by increasing his words, he utters foolishness from his mouth, for transgression is inevitable; therefore I say, “let your words be few.”²

footnotes:
² Scripture in Mishlei [17:28] states, “even a fool who is silent will be considered wise, [and] when he seals his lips [he will be considered] understanding.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:3:1)


**אֵין חֵפֶץ בַּכְּסִילִים.** אֵין חֵפֶץ לִפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בָּרְשָׁעִים הַנּוֹדְרִים וְאֵין מְשַׁלְּמִים:


**He has no desire in fools.** The Holy One, Blessed Is He, has no pleasure in the wicked who vow and do not pay.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:1)


**אַל־תִּתֵּן אֶת־פִּיךָ.** בְּנֶדֶר:


**Do not permit your mouth.** [By uttering] a vow.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:2)


**לַחֲטִיא אֶת־בְּשָׂרֶךָ.** שֶׁיִּפְקֹד הֶעָוֹן עַל־בָּנֶיךָ:


**To bring guilt on your flesh.** That He should visit the iniquity upon your children.³

footnotes:
³ According to Rashi, בשרך means children, as in, “and do not hide yourself from your kin [ומבשרך],” in Yeshayahu 58:7. Alternatively, it can be understood literally, i.e., do not let the vain vows made by your mouth to bring punishment upon your flesh [=yourself=בשרך]. See Maseches Shavuos 39a.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:3)


**וְאַל־תֹּאמַר לִפְנֵי הַמַּלְאָךְ.** שָׁלִיחַ הַבָּא לְתָבְעֲךָ צְדָקָה שֶׁפָּסַקְתָּ בְּרַבִּים:


**Nor say before the angel.** The representative who comes to demand of you the charity that you publicly pledged.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Alternatively, “an angel of God” who keeps and records all of man’s utterances (Ibn Ezra); or מלאך refers to “a Torah scholar” who is called upon to annul vows. (Sforno)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:4)


**כִּי שְׁגָגָה הִיא.** בִּשְׁגָגָה פְסַקְתִּיהָ. כְּסָבוּר הָיִיתִי שֶׁתִּהְיֶה יְכֹלֶת בְּיָדִי לִתֵּן:


**That it was an error.** I pledged it in error, I thought that I would have the ability to give.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:5)


**וְחִבֵּל אֶת־מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיךָ.** מִצְוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ בְיָדֶיךָ, שֶׁעָשִׂיתָ כְבָר, אִבַּדְתָּם. כַּךְ נִדְרָשׁ בַּמִּדְרָשׁ:


**And destroy the work of your hands.** [I.e.,] the commandments that were in your hands, that you had already performed, will cause them to be lost [=destroyed].⁵ It is expounded in this manner in the Midrash.

footnotes:
⁵ Alternatively, מעשה ידיך refers to the “children” of one who fails to fulfill his vows, who die as a result.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:5:6)


**עַל־קוֹלֶךָ.** בִּשְׁבִיל קוֹלֶךָ:


**By your voice.** Because of your voice.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:6:1)


**כִּי בְרֹב חֲלֹמוֹת וְגוֹ'.** אֲשֶׁר כָּל־מַה שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ לְךָ חֲלוֹמוֹת וּנְבִיאֵי הֶבֶל וּדְבָרִים הַרְבֵּה לִפְרשׁ מֵאֵת הַמָּקוֹם:


**For in the multitude of dreams, etc.** For all that they tell you — dreams, false prophets, and idle chatter — to separate from the Omnipresent.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:6:2)


**כִּי אֶת־הָאֱלֹהִים יְרָא.** הֲרֵי "כִּי" מְשַׁמֵּשׁ בִּלְשׁוֹן "אֶלָּא". אַל תִּשְׁמַע אֶל הַחֲלוֹמוֹת, אֶלָּא אֶת־הָאֱלֹהִים יְרָא:


**Man should fear God.** [The word] כִּי is an expression of “rather,” [i.e.,] do not obey the dreams, rather, “fear God.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:1)


**אִם־עשֶׁק רָשׁ וְגֵזֶל מִשְׁפָּט וָצֶדֶק וְגוֹ'.** אִם תִּרְאֶה בַמְּדִינָה שֶׁהֵם עוֹשְׁקִים אֶת־הָרָשִׁים וְגוֹזְלִים אֶת־הַמִּשְׁפָּט וְאֶת הַצֶּדֶק:


**Oppression of the poor, perversion of justice and righteousness, etc.** If you see in the province that they oppress the poor and deprive justice and righteousness.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:2)


**אַל־תִּתְמַהּ.** עַל חֶפְצוֹ שֶׁל מָקוֹם כְּשֶׁיָּבִיא עֲלֵיהֶם רָעָה:


**Do not be astonished.** About the will of the Omnipresent, when He exacts retribution upon them.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:3)


**כִּי גָבֹהַּ מֵעַל גָּבֹהַּ שֹׁמֵר.** וְרוֹאֶה אֶת־מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם, וּגְבוֹהִים יֵשׁ עֲלֵיהֶם הָעוֹשִׂים שְׁלִיחוּתוֹ שֶׁל מָקוֹם וְיָדָם תַּקִּיפָה לִפָּרַע מֵהֶם:


**For One higher than the high watches.** And sees their deeds, and there are higher ones⁶ over them who carry out the mission of the Omnipresent, and their hand is strong to punish them.

footnotes:
⁶ I.e., angels.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:4)


**וְגֵזֶל מִשְׁפָּט.** גָּזֵל שֶׁל מִשְׁפָּט. לְפִי שֶׁהוּא דָבוּק, הוּא נָקוּד "גֵּזֶל", פַּתָּח קָטָן, שֶׁאִם לֹא הָיָה דָבוּק, הָיָה נָקוּד "גָּזֵל" קָמַץ. עִנְיָן אַחֵר: אִם תִּרְאֶה שֶׁהֵם עוֹשְׁקִים רָשִׁים וְגוֹזְלִים מִשְׁפָּט, וְרוֹאֶה אַתָּה צֶדֶק בָּא לָעִיר, שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מַשְׁפִּיעַ לָהֶם טוֹבָה, וְאֵינוֹ נִפְרָע מֵהֶם, אַל תִּתְמַהּ עַל חֶפְצוֹ שֶׁל מָקוֹם, כִּי כֵן דַּרְכּוֹ, לְהַאֲרִיךְ אַפּוֹ:


**Perversion of justice.** Deprivation of justice. Since it is in the construct [i.e., possessive] state, גֵּזֶל is vowelized with a small *patach* [=*segol*],⁷ for were it not possessive, גָּזֵל would be vowelized with a *kametz*. Another explanation: If you see that they oppress the poor and deprive [them of] justice, and you see charity coming toward the city, [i.e.,] that the Holy One, Blessed Is He, lavishes goodness upon them and does not mete out retribution upon them, do not wonder about the will of the Omnipresent, for so is His custom, to be slow to anger.

footnotes:
⁷ Apparently Rashi’s text is not like our edition where גזל is vowelized with a tzeireh.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:5)


**כִּי גָבֹהַּ מֵעַל גָּבֹהַּ שֹׁמֵר.** מַמְתִּין עַד שֶׁתִּתְמַלֵּא סְאָתָם:


**For One higher than the high watches.** He waits until their measure is filled.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:7:6)


**וּגְבֹהִים.** יֵשׁ לוֹ עֲלֵיהֶם לִפָּרַע מֵהֶם בְּבֹא עֵת פְּקֻדָּתָם, כְּמוֹ: "לֹא־תִשְׁמוֹר עַל־חַטָּאתִי"; "שֹׁמֵר אֱמֻנִים"; "שָׁמַר אֶת־הַדָּבָר":


**And there are high ones.** He has [many agents] over them to punish them when their time of visitation arrives, as in, “you do not wait תִּשְׁמוֹר for my sin,”⁸ [and as in,] “awaiting שֹׁמֵר the realization,”⁹ [and as in,] “awaited שָׁמַר the matter.”¹⁰

footnotes:
⁸ Iyov 14:16.
⁹ Yeshayahu 26:2.
¹⁰ Bereishis 37:11. God waits, but ultimately He will punish them in the proper time.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:8:1)


**וְיִתְרוֹן אֶרֶץ בַּכֹּל הוּא.** וְיִתְרוֹן שֶׁל יוֹשְׁבֵי הָאָרֶץ, שֶׁהֵם מִתְגָּאִים וּמַכְעִיסִים לַמָּקוֹם, בַּכֹּל הוּא. בְּכָל־דָּבָר הוּא עוֹשֶׂה שְׁלִיחוּתוֹ לִפָּרַע וַאֲפִילוּ עַל יְדֵי יִתּוּשִׁין, כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁעָשָׂה לְטִיטוּס:


**The profit of the earth is desired by all.** And the loftiness of the earth’s dwellers, who are haughty and provoke the Omnipresent; “is desired by all,” in everything, he performs His mission to punish them, even through gnats, as He did to Titus.¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ See Maseches Gittin 56b for details of Titus’ punishment. When God created His world, He did not create a single thing without a purpose, according to the Gemara in Maseches Shabbos 77b.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:8:2)


**מֶלֶךְ לְשָׂדֶה נֶעֱבָד.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא נַעֲשֶׂה פוֹעֵל לְצִיּוֹן לִתְבֹּעַ עֶלְבּוֹנָהּ מִמַּחֲרִיבֶיהָ, וּלְשַׁלֵּם שְׂכָרָהּ לְבוֹנֶיהָ:


**The king himself is subject to the soil.** The Holy One, Blessed Is He, became subservient to Tzion to demand from her destroyers for [their] mistreatment [of Tzion], and to reward her builders.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:1)


**אֹהֵב כֶּסֶף לֹא־יִשְׂבַּע כֶּסֶף.** אוֹהֵב מִצְוֹת לֹא יִשְׂבַּע מֵהֶם:


**He who loves money will never be satisfied with money.** Whoever loves [to perform God’s] commandments never has his fill of them.¹²

footnotes:
¹² See the Gemara in Maseches Makkos 10a which interprets that this verse is referring to Moshe.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:2)


**וּמִי אוֹהֵב בֶּהָמוֹן.** מִצְוֹת רַבּוֹת:


**And he who loves abundance.** Of many commandments


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:3)


**לֹא תְבוּאָה.** וְאֵין בְּאֶחָד מֵהֶם מִצְוָה מְסֻיֶּמֶת וְנִכֶּרֶת, כְּגוֹן בִּנְיָן בֵּית־הַמִּקְדָּשׁ וּבֵית הַכְּנֶסֶת וְסֵפֶר תּוֹרָה נָאֶה:


**Will have no produce.** And among them none has a specific and distinguishable commandment, e.g., the building of the Beis Hamikdosh or a synagogue, or [writing] a beautiful Torah scroll.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:4)


**גַּם־זֶה הָבֶל.** כַּךְ נִדְרָשִׁים שְׁנֵי מִקְרָאוֹת הַלָּלוּ בַּמִּדְרָשׁ, וְעוֹד פָּנִים אֲחֵרִים הֲגוּנִים, אַךְ זֶה הִקְדַּמְתִּי לְפִי שֶׁהוּא מֵעִנְיַן "וּגְבֹהִים עֲלֵיהֶם", שֶׁסְּמָכָן הַכָּתוּב יָחַד.


**This too is vanity.** So are these two verses expounded upon in the Midrash, and there are other suitable interpretations, but I stated this one first because it is related to the matter of, “and there are higher ones over them,” which Scripture juxtaposed to them.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:5)


דָּבָר אַחֵר: **וְיִתְרוֹן אֶרֶץ בַּכֹּל הוּא.** שְׂכַר עֲבוֹדַת הָאֲדָמָה חָשׁוּב הוּא עַל הַכֹּל, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ הוּא מֶלֶךְ, צָּרִיךְ הוּא לִהְיוֹת נֶעֱבַד לְשָׂדֶה; אִם עָשְׂתָה הָאָרֶץ פֵּרוֹת, יֵשׁ לוֹ מַה יֹּאכַל, וְאִם לַאו, מֵת בְּרָעָב:


Another explanation: **The profit of the earth is desired by all.** The reward for tilling the soil is most important of all, for even the king must be subservient to the field; if the earth produces fruits, he will eat, otherwise, he dies from hunger.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:6)


**אֹהֵב כֶּסֶף לֹא יִשְׂבַּע כֶּסֶף.** לֹא יֹאכַל כֶּסֶף:


**He who loves money will never be satisfied with money.** He will not eat money.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:7)


**וּמִי אֹהֵב בֶּהָמוֹן.** בְּמָמוֹן:


**And he who loves abundance.** Of money.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:8)


**לֹא תְבוּאָה.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ אוֹסֵף לוֹ פֵרוֹת, גַּם־זֶה הָבֶל.


**Will have no produce.** That he does not gather for himself produce, this too is vanity.¹³

footnotes:
¹³ Alternatively, “one who loves [an] abundance” of slaves, maidservants and other household members, “will have no produce,” i.e., he will be unable to sustain his household staff, for they will consume everything (Metsudas Dovid).


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:9)


דָּבָר אַחֵר: **וְיִתְרוֹן אֶרֶץ בַּכֹּל הוּא.** שְׂכָרָן שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכָל־דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה הוּא, בֵּין בְּמִקְרָא, בֵּין בְּמִשְׁנָה, בֵּין בִּגְמָרָא:


Another explanation: **The profit of the earth is desired by all.** The reward of Yisroel is in all aspects of Torah, be it in Scripture, in Mishnah, or in Gemara.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:10)


**מֶלֶךְ לְשָׂדֶה נֶעֱבָד.** מֶלֶךְ בְּמִקְרָא וּבְמִשְׁנָה עוֹדֶנּוּ צָרִיךְ לִהְיוֹת נֶעֱבָד לְבַעַל גְּמָרָא, שֶׁהוּא מְסַדֵּר לְפָנָיו הוֹרָאוֹת אִסּוּר וְהֶתֵּר, טֻמְאָה וְטָהֳרָה, וְדִינִין:


**The king himself is subject to the soil.** If the king is well versed in Scripture and Mishnah, he must still be subservient to the one well-versed in Gemara, because he arranges before him the practical decisions regarding prohibition and permissibility, ritual impurity and purity, and practical laws.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:11)


**אוֹהֵב כֶּסֶף.** אוֹהֵב תּוֹרָה אֵינוֹ שָׂבֵעַ בָּהּ:


**He who loves money.** Whoever loves Torah [study], never has his fill with it.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:12)


**וְאֹהֵב בֶּהָמוֹן.** תּוֹרָה:


**And he who loves abundance.** Torah [study].


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:9:13)


**לֹא תְבוּאָה.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ מִקְרָא וּמִשְׁנָה וְאֵין לוֹ גְמָרָא, מָה הֲנָאָה יֵשׁ לוֹ? כָּל־אֵלּוּ בְּ"וַיִּקְרָא רַבָּה":


**Will have no produce.** One who has [knowledge of] Scripture and Mishnah, but has no [knowledge of] Gemara, what benefit does he have? All these are [expounded] in Vayikra Rabbah.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:1)


**בִּרְבוֹת הַטּוֹבָה.** כְּשֶׁיִּשְׂרָאֵל מְטִיבִין מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם:


**As goods increase.** When the [Bnei] Yisroel improve their deeds.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:2)


**רַבּוּ אוֹכְלֶיהָ.** מַתַּן שְׂכַר הַמִּצְוֹת:


**So do those who consume them.** The giving of the reward for [fulfilling] the commandments.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:3)


**וּמַה־כִּשְׁרוֹן לִבְעָלֶיהָ.** לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּכָל הַטָּבַת מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם:


**And what advantage has the owner.** [I.e.,] to the Holy One , Blessed Is He, with all the improvement of their deeds.¹⁴

footnotes:
¹⁴ Alternatively, “and what good is the owner’s skill [=כשרון],” as in 2:21 above.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:4)


**כִּי אִם־רְאוּת עֵינָיו.** שֶׁרוֹאֶה שֶׁהֵם כְּפוּפִים לוֹ וְנַחַת רוּחַ לְפָנָיו, שֶׁאָמַר וְנַעֲשָׂה רְצוֹנוֹ וְכֵן לְעִנְיַן הַקָרְבָּנוֹת:


**Except beholding them with his eyes.** That He sees that they are subservient to Him, and He has satisfaction, that He commanded and His will was done, and so is the matter concerning the sacrifices.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:5)


**בִּרְבוֹת הַטּוֹבָה רַבּוּ אוֹכְלֶיהָ.** שֶׁמְבִיאִים נְדָבוֹת הַרְבֵּה רָבּוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים אוֹכְלֶיהָ:


**As goods increase so do those who consume them.** When they bring many freewill offerings, the *kohanim* who eat it increase.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:6)


**וּמַה־כִּשְׁרוֹן לִבְעָלֶיהָ.** לִפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**And what advantage has the owner.** Before the Holy One, Blessed Is He.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:10:7)


**כִּי אִם־רְאוּת עֵינָיו.** שֶׁאָמַר וְנַעֲשָׂה רְצוֹנוֹ:


**Except beholding them with his eyes.** That He commanded, and His will was done.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:11:1)


**מְתוּקָה שְׁנַת הָעֹבֵד.** עוֹבֵד הָאֲדָמָה יָשֵׁן, וְעָרְבָה שְׁנָתוֹ עָלָיו, בֵּין שֶׁהוּא אוֹכֵל מְעַט וּבֵין שֶׁהוּא אוֹכֵל הַרְבֵּה, כִּי כְּבָר הֻרְגַּל בְּכָךְ:


**Sweet is the sleep of the laborer.** One who tills the soil sleeps, and he enjoys his sleep, whether he eats little or a lot, for he is already used to it.¹⁵

footnotes:
¹⁵ The Midrash relates that when Rabbi Boon died, Rabbi Zeira incorporated this verse, “sweet is the sleep of the laborer,” in his eulogy for Rabbi Boon who was only twenty-eight years old at his death. He said that Rabbi Boon accomplished in his life what would normally take one hundred years.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:11:2)


**וְהַשָּׂבָע לֶעָשִׁיר אֵינֶנּוּ מַנִיחַ לוֹ לִישׁוֹן.** וְשׂבַע נְכָסִים שֶׁל עָשִׁיר בַעַל פְּרַקְמַטְיָאוֹת הַרְבֵּה אֵינוֹ מַנִּיחַ לוֹ לִישׁוֹן, כָּל־הַלַּיְלָה מְהַרְהֵר בָּהֶן. דָּבָר אַחֵר: "מְתוּקָה שְׁנַת הָעוֹבֵד" אֶת־הָאֱלֹהִים, "אִם מְעַט" יְמֵי שָׁנָיו "וְאִם הַרְבֵּה" יְמֵי שָׁנָיו, יֹאכַל שְׂכָרוֹ הַמּוּעָט כִּמְרֻבֶּה. משֶׁה פִרְנֵס אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵל מ' שָׁנָה, וּשְׁמוּאֵל הַנָּבִיא פִרְנְסָם עֶשֶׂר שָׁנִים, וּשְׁקָּלָן הַכָּתוּב זֶה כָזֶה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן בְּכֹהֲנָיו וּשְׁמוּאֵל בְּקֹרְאֵי שְׁמוֹ וְגוֹ'". כַּךְ נִדְרָשׁ בְּתַנְחוּמָא:


**But the satiety of the rich will not permit him to sleep.** But the satiety of possessions of the rich man, who owns much merchandise, does not allow him to sleep because he thinks about it all night.¹⁶ Another explanation: The year שָׁנָה שֶׁל=[שְׁנַת of the one who serves God is sweet; whether he has a short life or a long life, he will partake of his reward, the one [who lives few years] like the one who [lives] many [years]. [E.g.,] Moshe led Yisroel forty years, and Shmuel the prophet led them ten years, and Scripture equates them one to another, as it is stated, “Moshe and Aharon among His *kohanim*, and Shmuel among those who invoke His name, etc.”¹⁷ So is it expounded upon in Tanchuma.

footnotes:
¹⁶ The same idea is expressed in Mishnayos Avos 2:7, “the more possessions, the more worry.”
¹⁷ Tehillim 99:6.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:11:3)


**וְהַשָּׂבָע לֶעָשִׁיר.** בַּעַל שְׁמוּעוֹת:


**But the satiety of the rich.** The one with many traditions.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:11:4)


**אֵינֶנּוּ מַנִּיחַ לוֹ לִישׁוֹן.** בַּקֶּבֶר, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "דּוֹבֵב שִׂפְתֵי יְשֵׁנִים". כָּל־תַּלְמִיד חָכָם שֶׁאוֹמְרִים דְּבַר שְׁמוּעָה מִפִּיו, שִׂפְתוֹתָיו דּוֹבְבוֹת בַּקָּבֶר:


**Will not permit him to sleep.** In the grave, as it is stated, “causing the lips of the sleeping to murmur.”¹⁸ Every Torah scholar, in whose name a traditional law is recited, his lips murmur in the grave.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁸ Shir Hashirim 7:10.
¹⁹ See Maseches Yevamos 97a.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:12:1)


**עשֶׁר שָׁמוּר לִבְעָלָיו לְרָעָתוֹ.** כְּעָשְׁרוֹ שֶׁל קֹרַח, שֶׁעַל יְדֵי כֵן נִתְגָּאָה וְיָרַד לִשְׁאוֹל:


**Wealth preserved by its owner to his own detriment.** Like the wealth of Korach, because of which he became haughty and descended into the grave.²⁰

footnotes:
²⁰ Alternatively, a person’s wealth jeopardizes his personal safety by exposing him to robbery, kidnap, or even death. (Sforno). Or, “wealth stored by its owner,” i.e., he stores his wealth and does not spend it to aid the needy, “to his own detriment,” i.e., resulting in the visitation of punishment upon him.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:13:1)


**וְאֵין בְּיָדוֹ מְאוּמָה.** אַף זְכוּת אָבוֹת:


**He will have nothing in his hand.** Not even the merit of his ancestors.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:14:1)


**וּמְאוּמָה לֹא יִשָּׂא בַעֲמָלוֹ.** כְּשֶׁיָּמוּת, לֹא יִשָּׂא בְיָדוֹ שׁוּם זְכוּת צְדָקָה שֶׁעָשָׂה בְמָמוֹנוֹ בְחַיָּיו:


**He will take nothing for his labor.** When he dies, he will not take with him any merit of charity that he did with his money during his lifetime.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:15:1)


**כָּל־עֻמַּת שֶׁבָּא.** אוֹתוֹ הַמָּמוֹן, כֵּן יֵלֵךְ:


**As he came.** That money, so will it go.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:16:1)


**וְחָלְיוֹ.** כְּמוֹ "וְחֹלִי", וְהַוָּ"ו יְתֵרָה, כְּמוֹ וָי"ו שֶׁל "חַיְתוֹ יָעַר":


**[He has] illness.** וְחָלְיוֹ means] the same as ‘וְחֹלִי,’ and the *vav* is superfluous, just as the *vav* of, “beast חַיְתוֹ of the forest.”²¹

footnotes:
²¹ Tehillim 104:20.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:17:1)


**לֶאֱכוֹל וְלִשְׁתּוֹת וְלִרְאוֹת טוֹבָה.** לַעֲסֹק בַּתּוֹרָה שֶׁהִיא לֶקַח טוֹב, וְאַל יְקַבֵּץ הוֹן רַב אֶלָּא בַחֵלֶק הַנִּתָּן לוֹ יִשְׂמַח, כִּי הוּא חֶלְקוֹ:


**For one to eat and drink and to enjoy the good.** To engage in Torah [study],²² which is a good doctrine; but he should not accumulate much wealth, rather he should rejoice with the portion given him, for that is his portion.

footnotes:
²² Rashi is consistent with the Midrash Aggadah he cited in 8:15 below, “that all mention of ‘eating and drinking’ in Koheles refers only to the study of Torah.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:18:1)


**וְהִשְׁלִיטוֹ לֶאֱכֹל מִמֶּנּוּ.** בְּחַיָּיו:


**And the power to enjoy them.** In his life.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:18:2)


**וְלָשֵׂאת אֶת־חֶלְקוֹ.** בְּמוֹתוֹ, שֶׁיְּזַכֵּהוּ לַעֲסֹק בַּתּוֹרָה וּבַמִּצְוֹת בְּחַיָּיו כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּקַבֵּל שָׂכָר:


**To take his share.** After his death, that he merited to engage in Torah [study] and in [the performance of] commandments during his life, which enabled him to receive reward.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:1)


**כִּי לֹא הַרְבֵּה.** שֶׁאֵין אֹרֶךְ יָמִים בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה:


**For there are not many.** For there is no longevity in this world.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:2)


**יִזְכֹּר אֶת־יְמֵי חַיָּיו.** כִּי מְעַט הֵם, וְלֹא הַרְבֵּה, וְלָמָּה יַטְרִיחַ לֶאֱסֹף הוֹן? יַטְרִיחַ בַּדָּבָר הָעוֹמֵד לוֹ לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא, בְּחַיָּיו:


**Let him remember the days of his life.** For they are few and not many; so why then should he bother to accumulate wealth? Let him labor with something that endures for him in the World to Come during his lifetime.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:3)


**כִּי הָאֱלֹהִים מַעֲנֶה וְגוֹ'.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עֵדוּת קְבוּעָה לָזֶה לְעוֹלָם:


**While God answers [provides] him, etc.** The Holy One, Blessed Is He, bears a permanent testimony about this eternally.²³

footnotes:
²³ According to Rashi מענה means, “bears testimony” as in, “you shall not bear false testimony [לא תענה] in Shemos 20:13. Alternatively, מענה means “answers” as in “may Adonoy answer [יענך] you,” in Tehillim 20:2. (Ibn Ezra)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:4)


**בְּשִׁמְחַת לִבּוֹ.** שֶׁשָּׂמֵחַ לַעֲשׂוֹת טוֹב בְּחַיָּיו. וְרָאִיתִי בַמִּדְרָשׁ, זֶה אֶלְקָנָה שֶׁהִדְּרִיךְ אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵל לַעֲלוֹת לְשִׁילֹה בָרְגָלִים, וּבַדֶּרֶךְ שֶׁמַּעֲלֶה אוֹתָן בְּשָׁנָה זוּ, לֹא הָיָה מַעֲלֶה אוֹתָן לַשָּׁנָה הָאַחֶרֶת, כְּדֵי לְפַרְסֵם הַדָּבָר וּלְהַרְגִּילָם, לְפִיכָךְ יִחֲסוֹ הַכָּתוּב: "וְעָלָה הָאִישׁ הַהוּא מֵעִירוֹ וְגוֹ'". וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי שֶׁהַמִּדְרָשׁ הַזֶּה סוֹף הַמִּקְרָא הוּא: "כִּי הָאֱלֹהִים מַעֲנֶה בְּשִׂמְחַת לִבּוֹ". זֶה אֶלְקָנָה, שֶׁקָּבַע לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עֵדוּת בַּכְּתוּבִים, וְהֵעִיד עָלָיו, "וְעָלָה הָאִישׁ הַהוּא מֵעִירוֹ":


**With the joy of his heart.** That he rejoiced to do good in his lifetime, and I saw in the Midrash, that this refers to Elkanah, who led Yisroel up to Shiloh on the festivals, and on the route that he brought them up on one year he did not bring them back the following year, in order to publicize the matter and to accustom them. Therefore, Scripture praises him, “This man would go up from his city, etc.”²⁴ I believe that this Midrash is at the end of the verse, “for God answers [provides] him with the joy of his heart.” This is Elkanah, whom the Holy One, Blessed Is He, established in Scripture, and testifies about him, “this man would go up from his city.”²⁵

footnotes:
²⁴ I Shmuel 1:3.
²⁵ Ibid.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:5)


**מַעֲנֶה.** נָקוּד פַּתָּח קָטָן; לְכַךְ אֲנִי מְפָרְשׁוֹ שֵׁם דָּבָר, כְּמוֹ, "כִּי אֵין מַעֲנֶה בְּפִי שְׁלשֶׁת הָאֲנָשִׁים:


**Answers [provides] him.** This is vowelized with a small *pasach* [=*segol*].. Therefore, I explain it as a noun, as in, “for there was no answer מַעֲנֶה in the mouth[s] of the three men.”²⁶

footnotes:
²⁶ Iyov 32:5.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 5:19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/5:19:6)


**בְּשִׂמְחַת לִבּוֹ.** שֶׁהָיָה שָׂמֵחַ בְּרָגֶל:


**With the joy of his heart.** That he was happy on the festival.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:1:1)


**וְרַבָּה הִיא עַל־הָאָדָם.** בְּהַרְבֵּה בְּנֵי אָדָם הִיא נוֹהֶגֶת:


**And it weighs heavily on man.** It is prevalent among mankind.¹

footnotes:
¹ It causes distress upon mankind.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:2:1)


**עשֶׁר וּנְכָסִים.** לְפִי פְשׁוּטוֹ, כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ:


**Wealth [and] possessions.** Its simple explanation, is its apparent meaning.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:2:2)


**וְלֹא יַשְׁלִיטֶנּוּ הָאֱלֹהִים לֶאֱכֹל מִמֶּנּוּ.** שֶׁיְּהֵא שָׂמֵחַ בְּחֶלְקוֹ, לִמְצֹא קוֹרַת רוּחַ בְעָשְׁרוֹ, כִּי שׁוֹאֵף לַעֲשֹׁק וּלְהַרְבּוֹת הוֹן, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר, "וְהוּא כַמָּוֶת וְלֹא יִשְׂבָּע", וְגַם לֹא יַשְׁלִיטֶנּוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת צְדָקָה לֶאֱכֹל הֵימֶנָּה לְעָתִיד, וְאִישׁ נָכְרִי יִטֹּל אוֹתוֹ מָמוֹן וְיַעֲשֶׂה בוֹ צְדָקָה וְיֵהָנֶה מִמֶּנּוּ. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה בְּדִבְרֵי תוֹרָה.


**Yet God does not give him the power to eat thereof.** That he should be happy with his portion to find satisfaction in his wealth, for he strives to oppress and to accumulate much wealth, as the matter is stated, “and he is like death and is never satisfied,”² and also He does not give him the power to perform charity, from which to eat in the future, but a stranger will take that money and perform charity and he will benefit from it.³ However, the Midrash Aggadah explains it as referring to Torah study.

footnotes:
² Chavakuk 2:5.
³ According to Targum, the verse is dealing with a person who dies on account of his sins without leaving heirs. His wife marries a stranger who enjoys the estate that he left.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:2:3)


**עשֶׁר וּנְכָסִים וְכָבוֹד.** מִקְרָא, מִשְׁנָה וְאַגָּדָה.


**Wealth, possessions and honor.** Scripture, Mishnah, and Aggadah.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:2:4)


**וְלֹא יַשְׁלִיטֶנּוּ.** שֶׁלֹּא זָכָה לִגְמָרָא, וּמִתּוֹךְ כַּךְ, אֵין הֲנָאָה מִמֶּנּוּ בְּשׁוּם דְּבַר הוֹרָאָה.


**Does not give him the power.** Because he did not achieve [knowledge of] Gemara, he consequently has no benefit from his studies in any practical decision making.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:2:5)


**כִּי אִישׁ נָכְרִי יֹאכְלֶנּוּ.** זֶה בַּעַל הַגְּמָרָא:


**For instead a stranger will enjoy them.** The one who is versed in Gemara.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:3:1)


**אִם יוֹלִיד אִישׁ מֵאָה.** בָּנִים:


**If a man begets a hundred [children].** Children.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ “A hundred” is figuratively speaking, it is an expression used to denote “many.” (Ibn Ezra)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:3:2)


**וְרַב שֶׁיִּהְיוּ יְמֵי שָׁנָיו.** (וְרַב הוֹן וְכָל־טוּב יְמֵי שָׁנָיו) שֶׁלּוֹ. "וְרַב" לְשׁוֹן דַּי. דַּי לְכָל־טוֹבָה:


**So that the days of his years are many.** (and much wealth and all goodness during the days of his life) His; וְרַב is an expression meaning “sufficient,” [i.e.,] a sufficient [=וְרַב degree of all goodness.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ Alternatively, ורב means “and honorable” (Metsudas Dovid). Or, “the length of his life is greater [=ורב] than the average.” (Ibn Ezra)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:3:3)


**וְנַפְשׁוֹ לֹא תִשְׂבַּע.** מֵאוֹתָהּ הַטּוֹבָה, שֶׁאֵינוֹ שָׂמֵחַ בְּחֶלְקוֹ לְהִתְקָרֵר רוּחוֹ בַּמֶּה שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ:


**And his soul is not sated.** From that goodness, for he is not happy with his portion, to be satisfied with what is his.⁶

footnotes:
⁶ According to the Midrash, this phrase refers to the soul which comes from Above and is not sated with physical pleasures. Rather, its needs are spiritual, e.g., studying Torah, performing mitzvos and doing good deeds.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:3:4)


**וְגַם קְבוּרָה לֹא הָיְתָה לּוֹ.** פְּעָמִים שֶׁנֶהֱרָג, וּכְלָבִים אוֹכְלִים אוֹתוֹ. וְכָל־הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלּוּ נִמְצְאוּ בְאַחְאָב, שֶׁהוֹלִיד בָּנִים הַרְבֵּה, וּמָמוֹנוֹ הִרְבָּה, וְהָיָה חוֹמֵד שֶׁל אֲחֵרִים, וְלֹא מָצָא קוֹרַת רוּחַ בְּמָמוֹנוֹ, וּכְלָבִים אֲכָלוּהוּ:


**And moreover he has no burial.** Sometimes he is slain, and dogs devour him. All these happened to Achav, he begot many children, and he had great wealth, but he coveted [the property of] others and was not satisfied with his wealth, and [after he was killed] dogs devoured him.⁷

footnotes:
⁷ According to the Midrash, only Achav’s blood was not buried, as Scripture states in I Melochim 22:38, “the dogs licked it up,” but his body was buried in Shomron [Ibid. Verse 37]. Alternatively, he did not have a proper burial, e.g., he was buried without burial shrouds (Ibn Ezra). Or, he was involved and focused on accumulating wealth to such a degree that one might think he would live forever and need “no burial.” (Ateres Shmuel)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:3:5)


**טוֹב מִמֶּנּוּ הַנָּפֶל.** שֶׁל אִשָּׁה, כִּי הַנֶּפֶל בַּהֶבֶל בָּא וְהָלַךְ, וְלֹא רָאָה טוֹבָה וְלֹא נִתְאַוָּה לָהּ, וְאֵין לוֹ לְהִצְטַעֵר:


**Better off than he is the stillborn child.** [The stillborn] of a woman, for the stillborn came in vanity and went, he neither saw good nor desired it; so he does not need to be distressed.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:6:1)


**וְאִלּוּ חָיָה.** וְאִם חָיָה אַלְפַּיִם שָׁנָה, מַה יִתְרוֹן לוֹ, הוֹאִיל וְטוֹבָה לֹא רָאָה? הֲלֹא סוֹפוֹ לָשׁוּב אֶל הֶעָפָר כִּשְׁאָר הָעֲנִיִּים?:


**Though he live.** And if he had lived two thousand years,⁸ what advantage would he have, since he did not experience any pleasure. Will he not ultimately return to the dust like all the poor people?

footnotes:
⁸ Alternatively, one million years [אלף פעמים=1000 x1000]. (Ibn Ezra)



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:7:1)


**כָּל־עֲמַל הָאָדָם.** בִּשְׁבִיל פִּיהוּ הוּא, שֶׁיֵּהָנֶה וְיֹּאכַל בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה וְהַבָּא, וְזֶה לֹא נֶהֱנָה בְחַיָּיו:


**All man’s labor.** Is for his mouth, that he should benefit and eat in this world and in the next, but this one had no benefit in his life.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:7:2)


**וְגַם־הַנֶּפֶשׁ לֹא תִמָּלֵא.** בִּתְמִיהָּ כְּלוֹמַר, וְזֶה אֲפִלּוּ תַאֲוָתוֹ לֹא מִלֵּא בַהֲנָאָה מֻעֶטֶת, כְּמוֹ, "תִּמְלָאֵמוֹ נַפְשִׁי", לְשׁוֹן הַשָּׂגַת תַּאֲוָה. וַאַחֲרֵי שֶׁכֵּן הוּא:


**Yet his appetite is never filled.** This is a question; i.e., but this one, did he not even gratify his desire with a small pleasure? This is similar to, “I will satisfy תִּמְלָאֵמוֹ my lust with them,”⁹ an expression of attaining a desire. And since this is so —

footnotes:
⁹ Shemos 15:9.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:8:1)


**מַה־יוֹתֵר.** לוֹ בְחַכְמָתוֹ מִשֶּׁאִם הָיָה כְסִיל:


**What advantage.** [What advantage does he have] with his wisdom, over what he would have if he were a fool.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:8:2)


**מַה־לֶּעָנִי.** חִסָּרוֹן מִן הֶעָשִׁיר שֶׁאֵין לוֹ קוֹרַת רוּחַ? גַּם הוּא יוֹדֵעַ לַהֲלֹךְ בָּאָרֶץ אֵצֶל הַחַיִּים. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **וְגַם הַנֶּפֶשׁ לֹא תִמָּלֵא** לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא עָשָׂה מַעֲשִׂים טוֹבִים בְּחַיָּיו:


**What disadvantage has the poor man.** Over the rich man who has no satisfaction. He too knows how to go along with the living. Another explanation: **Yet his appetite is never filled.** For the World to Come, for he did not perform good deeds in his lifetime.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:9:1)


**טוֹב מַרְאֵה עֵינַיִם מֵהֲלָךְ־נָפֶשׁ.** טוֹב הָיָה וְהֻכְשַׁר לָזֶה לִרְאוֹת עָשְׁרוֹ לְמַרְאֵה עֵינָיו מִמַּאֲכָל וּמִשְׁתֶּה הַהוֹלֵךְ בְּנָפֶשׁ.


**Better is what the eyes actually see than the imagination of the soul.** It would have been better and more proper for this one to gaze upon his wealth [to enjoy] what his eyes see, than [by investing his wealth] in food and drink that nourish the soul.


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ecclesiastes/r/6:9:2)


דָּבָר אַחֵר: **טוֹב מַרְאֵה עֵינַיִם מֵהֲלָךְ־נָפֶשׁ.** טוֹב הָיָה לָזֶה וְהֻכְשַׁר לָלֶכֶת אַחַר עֵינָיו לִגְזֹל וְלַעֲשֹׁק מֵהִלּוּךְ נַפְשׁוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא נָתַן לֵב הֵיכָן נַפְשׁוֹ תֵלֵךְ כְּשֶׁיָּמוּת:


Another explanation: **Better is what the eyes actually see than the imagination of the soul.** It would have better and more proper for this one to follow his eyes, to rob and to oppress, rather than [being mindful of] the course of his soul, that he did not care where his soul will go when he dies.¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ Alternatively, it would be better for a person to actually see where the soul of the righteous goes and where the soul of the wicked goes after their death. He would realize the difference and mend his ways. (Metsudas Dovid)


###### Rashi on Ecclesiastes 6:9:3
